Despite its small footprint, PanLUNA matches or exceeds models up to 57$\times$ larger: 81.21% balanced accuracy on TUAB abnormal EEG detection and state-of-the-art 0.7416 balanced accuracy on HMC multimodal sleep staging. Quantization-aware training with INT8 weights recovers $\geq$96% of full-precision performance, and deployment on the GAP9 ultra-low-power RISC-V microcontroller for wearables achieves 325.6 ms latency and 18.8 mJ per 10-second, 12-lead ECG inference, and 1.206 s latency at 68.65 mJ for multimodal 5-channel sleep staging over 30-second epochs.

</details>


### [114] [Soft Tournament Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04328)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Soft Tournament Equilibrium (STE)框架，用于评估存在循环优势关系的智能体，通过可微分方法计算集合值锦标赛解，替代传统线性排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的通用智能体评估面临挑战，因为智能体间的交互具有非传递性（A胜B，B胜C，C胜A）。传统强制线性排序的方法会产生误导和不稳定结果，需要更合适的理论基础。

Method: 提出Soft Tournament Equilibrium (STE)框架：1) 从成对比较数据学习概率锦标赛模型；2) 使用新颖的可微分算子（soft reachability和soft covering）计算Top Cycle和Uncovered Set的连续模拟；3) 输出带校准成员分数的核心智能体集合。

Result: 建立了STE的理论基础，证明其在零温度极限下与经典解的一致性（Condorcet包含性质），分析了稳定性和样本复杂度。设计了合成和真实世界基准的验证实验协议。

Conclusion: 该工作为通用智能体评估提供了更合适和稳健的理论基础，从不稳定排名转向稳定的集合值均衡，重新定位评估范式。

Abstract: The evaluation of general-purpose artificial agents, particularly those based on large language models, presents a significant challenge due to the non-transitive nature of their interactions. When agent A defeats B, B defeats C, and C defeats A, traditional ranking methods that force a linear ordering can be misleading and unstable. We argue that for such cyclic domains, the fundamental object of evaluation should not be a ranking but a set-valued core, as conceptualized in classical tournament theory. This paper introduces Soft Tournament Equilibrium (STE), a differentiable framework for learning and computing set-valued tournament solutions directly from pairwise comparison data. STE first learns a probabilistic tournament model, potentially conditioned on rich contextual information. It then employs novel, differentiable operators for soft reachability and soft covering to compute continuous analogues of two seminal tournament solutions: the Top Cycle and the Uncovered Set. The output is a set of core agents, each with a calibrated membership score, providing a nuanced and robust assessment of agent capabilities. We develop the theoretical foundation for STE to prove its consistency with classical solutions in the zero-temperature limit, which establishes its Condorcet-inclusion properties, and analyzing its stability and sample complexity. We specify an experimental protocol for validating STE on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. This work aims to provide a complete, standalone treatise that re-centers general-agent evaluation on a more appropriate and robust theoretical foundation, moving from unstable rankings to stable, set-valued equilibria.

</details>


### [115] [Domain-Contextualized Inference: A Computable Graph Architecture for Explicit-Domain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04344)
*Chao Li,Yuru Wang,Chunyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种计算基板无关的推理架构，将领域作为显式的一类计算参数，实现领域范围剪枝、基板无关执行和透明推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理系统通常与特定计算基板（如符号、神经、向量）紧密耦合，缺乏领域作为显式计算参数，导致搜索空间大、跨基板执行困难、推理过程不透明。

Method: 建立五层架构：领域层、查询层、基板层、操作层、验证层；定义三种领域计算模式（链索引、路径遍历、向量引导计算）；设计基板无关接口（查询、扩展、桥接）；提出可靠性条件C1-C4和三类故障模式。

Result: 实现领域范围剪枝，将每查询搜索空间从O(N)降至O(N/K)；支持符号、神经、向量和混合基板的统一执行；建立透明推理链，每个步骤携带评估上下文；通过PHQ-9临床推理案例验证。

Conclusion: 贡献是架构性的而非逻辑性的，形式化了包含操作语义、复杂度界限、单子结构、基板转换和边界条件的计算理论，为多基板推理系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We establish a computation-substrate-agnostic inference architecture in which domain is an explicit first-class computational parameter. This produces domain-scoped pruning that reduces per-query search space from O(N) to O(N/K), substrate-independent execution over symbolic, neural, vector, and hybrid substrates, and transparent inference chains where every step carries its evaluative context. The contribution is architectural, not logical. We formalize the computational theory across five dimensions: a five-layer architecture; three domain computation modes including chain indexing, path traversal as Kleisli composition, and vector-guided computation as a substrate transition; a substrate-agnostic interface with three operations Query, Extend, Bridge; reliability conditions C1 to C4 with three failure mode classes; and validation through a PHQ-9 clinical reasoning case study. The computational theory including operational semantics, complexity bounds, monad structure, substrate transitions, and boundary conditions is the contribution of this paper.

</details>


### [116] [RoboPhD: Evolving Diverse Complex Agents Under Tight Evaluation Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04347)
*Andrew Borthwick,Stephen Ash,Anthony Galczak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了三种LLM引导的进化优化算法（Elo锦标赛选择、帕累托选择、贪婪爬山法）在固定评估预算下的性能，发现RoboPhD的Elo锦标赛选择在多数任务上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM引导的智能体进化系统（如GEPA、Autoresearch）快速发展，面临一个核心问题：在相同信息、种子智能体和目标下，哪种优化算法能在固定评估预算下获得最佳结果？这个问题在评估成本高昂时尤为重要。

Method: 1. 系统比较三种优化范式：Elo锦标赛选择（RoboPhD）、帕累托选择（GEPA）、贪婪爬山法（Autoresearch）；2. 在四个基准测试（抽象推理、云调度、SQL生成、金融QA）上进行实验；3. 采用固定评估预算（1,500次评估）；4. RoboPhD引入无验证进化：使用训练数据上的Elo竞争同时评估智能体和驱动进化；5. 所有系统接收带有诊断print()语句的种子智能体，允许进化发展自检测功能。

Result: 1. 在默认配置下，RoboPhD在四个基准测试中的三个上优于GEPA和Autoresearch；2. 仅在最简单的任务上落后（获胜方案来自Autoresearch，代码少于90行）；3. 在ARC-AGI任务中，RoboPhD将22行种子智能体进化为1,013行多策略系统，准确率从27.8%提升至65.8%（使用Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite作为求解器）。

Conclusion: RoboPhD的Elo锦标赛选择方法在多数复杂任务中表现最优，证明了无验证进化策略的有效性。作者将RoboPhD作为多功能工具包以MIT许可证发布，提供简单的optimize_anything() API用于进化各种复杂智能体。

Abstract: 2026 has brought an explosion of interest in LLM-guided evolution of agentic artifacts, with systems like GEPA and Autoresearch demonstrating that LLMs can iteratively improve prompts, code, and agent architectures across diverse domains. As adoption accelerates, a central question emerges: given the same information, the same seed agent, and the same objective, which optimization algorithm yields the best results under the same evaluation budget? This question becomes critical when evaluations are expensive, such as when they require human judgment or multiple LLM calls.
  We present the first systematic comparison of three optimization paradigms -- Elo tournament selection (RoboPhD), Pareto-based selection (GEPA), and greedy hill-climbing (Autoresearch) -- across four benchmarks spanning abstract reasoning, cloud scheduling, SQL generation, and financial QA, all under a fixed budget of 1,500 evaluations. RoboPhD introduces validation-free evolution: instead of splitting the budget between training and validation, it uses Elo competition on training data to simultaneously evaluate agents and drive evolution. All three systems receive seed agents with diagnostic print() statements that evolution can grow, enabling self-instrumenting agents that develop increasingly informative diagnostics for the benefit of their evolutionary successors.
  Using a single default configuration, RoboPhD outperforms both GEPA and Autoresearch on three of four benchmarks, losing only on the simplest task, where the winning solution (from our Autoresearch adaptation) required under 90 lines of code. On ARC-AGI, RoboPhD evolves a 22-line seed agent into a 1,013-line multi-strategy system, improving accuracy from 27.8% to 65.8% using Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite as the solver. We release RoboPhD as a versatile toolkit under the MIT license with a simple optimize_anything() API for evolving diverse complex agents.

</details>


### [117] [REAM: Merging Improves Pruning of Experts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04356)
*Saurav Jha,Maryam Hashemzadeh,Ali Saheb Pasand,Ali Parviz,Min-Joong Lee,Boris Knyazev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REAM是一种新的MoE LLM压缩方法，通过分组合并专家权重而非剪枝，在保持原始性能方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大语言模型参数庞大（数百亿），部署时面临显著内存挑战。传统方法如权重剪枝和量化存在性能损失问题，现有REAP方法剪枝专家会损害模型能力。

Method: 提出Router-weighted Expert Activation Merging (REAM)方法，不删除专家而是将专家分组并合并其权重，更好地保留原始模型性能。

Result: 在多个MoE LLM模型上评估REAM，发现MC和GEN性能存在权衡关系，取决于校准数据的混合比例。通过控制通用、数学和编程数据的混合，REAM在Pareto前沿上通常优于基线方法，在许多情况下与原始未压缩模型相当。

Conclusion: REAM作为一种新的MoE LLM压缩方法，通过合并而非剪枝专家权重，在保持模型性能方面优于现有压缩技术，为大型MoE模型的部署提供了有效的内存优化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models (LLMs) are among the top-performing architectures. The largest models, often with hundreds of billions of parameters, pose significant memory challenges for deployment. Traditional approaches to reduce memory requirements include weight pruning and quantization. Motivated by the Router-weighted Expert Activation Pruning (REAP) that prunes experts, we propose a novel method, Router-weighted Expert Activation Merging (REAM). Instead of removing experts, REAM groups them and merges their weights, better preserving original performance. We evaluate REAM against REAP and other baselines across multiple MoE LLMs on diverse multiple-choice (MC) question answering and generative (GEN) benchmarks. Our results reveal a trade-off between MC and GEN performance that depends on the mix of calibration data. By controlling the mix of general, math and coding data, we examine the Pareto frontier of this trade-off and show that REAM often outperforms the baselines and in many cases is comparable to the original uncompressed models.

</details>


### [118] [Decocted Experience Improves Test-Time Inference in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04373)
*Maohao Shen,Kaiwen Zha,Zexue He,Zhang-Wei Hong,Siru Ouyang,J. Jon Ryu,Prasanna Sattigeri,Suhas Diggavi,Gregory Wornell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将上下文作为提升LLM性能的补充扩展维度，研究如何通过经验构建更好的输入来引导推理，强调"提炼经验"是关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法（如增加推理长度、采样或搜索）虽然能提升LLM性能，但对于复杂推理和智能体任务，单纯增加计算成本高且可能导致次优探索。需要探索更高效的性能提升途径。

Method: 提出将上下文作为扩展维度，系统研究如何从经验中构建更好的输入来引导推理。重点分析经验增强型智能体，研究如何从经验中提取上下文、性能如何随经验积累而扩展、良好上下文的特征以及支持上下文构建的最佳数据结构。

Result: 识别出"提炼经验"作为有效上下文构建的关键机制：从经验中提取本质、组织成连贯结构、检索关键信息以构建有效上下文。在数学推理、网页浏览和软件工程等任务上验证了发现。

Conclusion: 上下文是提升LLM性能的重要扩展维度，通过经验构建的有效上下文能显著改善复杂推理和智能体任务的性能，而提炼经验是实现这一目标的核心机制。

Abstract: There is growing interest in improving LLMs without updating model parameters. One well-established direction is test-time scaling, where increased inference-time computation (e.g., longer reasoning, sampling, or search) is used to improve performance. However, for complex reasoning and agentic tasks, naively scaling test-time compute can substantially increase cost and still lead to wasted budget on suboptimal exploration. In this paper, we explore \emph{context} as a complementary scaling axis for improving LLM performance, and systematically study how to construct better inputs that guide reasoning through \emph{experience}. We show that effective context construction critically depends on \emph{decocted experience}. We present a detailed analysis of experience-augmented agents, studying how to derive context from experience, how performance scales with accumulated experience, what characterizes good context, and which data structures best support context construction. We identify \emph{decocted experience} as a key mechanism for effective context construction: extracting essence from experience, organizing it coherently, and retrieving salient information to build effective context. We validate our findings across reasoning and agentic tasks, including math reasoning, web browsing, and software engineering.

</details>


### [119] [Optimizing Service Operations via LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04383)
*Yanyuan Wang,Xiaowei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LLM驱动的多智能体仿真框架，用于优化服务运营，通过将设计选择嵌入提示词来建模决策依赖的不确定性，开发在线学习算法优化稳态性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务系统性能依赖于参与者对设计选择的响应，但由于人类行为的复杂性，建模这些响应非常困难。需要一种能够有效模拟人类决策行为的方法来优化服务运营设计

Method: 引入LLM驱动的多智能体仿真框架，将问题建模为具有决策依赖不确定性的随机优化问题。设计选择嵌入提示词中，通过提取LLM生成文本中的关键数值信息，将不确定性建模为受控马尔可夫链。开发在线学习算法，在单次仿真运行中同时构建零阶梯度估计并更新设计参数以优化稳态性能，并整合方差减少技术

Result: 在可持续供应链应用中，该方法优于基准方法，包括黑盒优化、使用LLM作为数值求解器或角色扮演系统设计者。在基于真实行为数据的最优竞赛设计案例研究中，LLM-MAS既能作为已知设计的成本效益评估器，又能作为探索工具，发现传统方法忽略的优秀设计

Conclusion: LLM-MAS框架为服务运营优化提供了一种有效方法，能够建模复杂的人类行为响应，并通过在线学习算法优化设计参数，在实际应用中展现出优于传统方法的性能

Abstract: Service system performance depends on how participants respond to design choices, but modeling these responses is hard due to the complexity of human behavior. We introduce an LLM-powered multi-agent simulation (LLM-MAS) framework for optimizing service operations. We pose the problem as stochastic optimization with decision-dependent uncertainty: design choices are embedded in prompts and shape the distribution of outcomes from interacting LLM-powered agents. By embedding key numerical information in prompts and extracting it from LLM-generated text, we model this uncertainty as a controlled Markov chain. We develop an on-trajectory learning algorithm that, on a single simulation run, simultaneously constructs zeroth-order gradient estimates and updates design parameters to optimize steady-state performance. We also incorporate variance reduction techniques. In a sustainable supply chain application, our method outperforms benchmarks, including blackbox optimization and using LLMs as numerical solvers or as role-playing system designers. A case study on optimal contest design with real behavioral data shows that LLM-MAS is both as a cost-effective evaluator of known designs and an exploratory tool that can uncover strong designs overlooked by traditional approaches.

</details>


### [120] [Automatically Generating Hard Math Problems from Hypothesis-Driven Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04386)
*Jiayu Fu,Mourad Heddaya,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于AI生成假设的数学基准测试生成管道，能够自动识别LLM在特定数学概念和技能上的弱点，并针对这些弱点生成新的基准测试问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准测试大多需要大量人工努力且难以扩展，无法跟上LLM发展速度或提供新实例来缓解过拟合问题。现有自动生成方法很少关注识别LLM易错的特定数学概念和技能，且大多只能生成特定类别的基准测试。

Method: 提出新的数学基准测试生成管道：1) 使用AI生成假设来识别LLM在哪些特定数学概念和技能上表现不佳；2) 针对这些弱点生成新的基准测试问题。

Result: 实验表明假设准确性与生成问题的难度正相关：基于最准确假设生成的问题将Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct的准确率降至45%，而原始MATH基准测试上为77%。该管道具有高度适应性，可应用于数学以外的领域探索LLM能力。

Conclusion: 该管道能够自动识别LLM弱点并生成针对性基准测试，是研究LLM在不同领域表现的有价值工具，解决了现有基准测试的可扩展性和针对性不足的问题。

Abstract: Numerous math benchmarks exist to evaluate LLMs' mathematical capabilities. However, most involve extensive manual effort and are difficult to scale. Consequently, they cannot keep pace with LLM development or easily provide new instances to mitigate overfitting. Some researchers have proposed automatic benchmark generation methods, but few focus on identifying the specific math concepts and skills on which LLMs are error-prone, and most can only generate category-specific benchmarks. To address these limitations, we propose a new math benchmark generation pipeline that uses AI-generated hypotheses to identify the specific math concepts and skills that LLMs struggle with, and then generates new benchmark problems targeting these weaknesses. Experiments show that hypothesis accuracy positively correlates with the difficulty of the generated problems: problems generated from the most accurate hypotheses reduce Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct's accuracy to as low as 45%, compared to 77% on the original MATH benchmark. Furthermore, our pipeline is highly adaptable and can be applied beyond math to explore a wide range of LLM capabilities, making it a valuable tool for investigating how LLMs perform across different domains.

</details>


### [121] [MolDA: Molecular Understanding and Generation via Large Language Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04403)
*Seohyeon Shin,HanJun Choi,Jun-Hyung Park,Hongkook Kim,Mansu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MolDA：一种新型多模态分子生成框架，用掩码扩散模型替代传统自回归骨干，通过双向迭代去噪确保全局结构一致性和化学有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归骨干的多模态分子架构存在局限性：严格的从左到右归纳偏差不适用于生成化学有效分子，难以处理非局部全局约束（如环闭合），且在序列生成过程中容易累积结构错误

Method: 1. 用离散大语言扩散模型替代传统自回归骨干；2. 使用混合图编码器提取全面结构表示，捕获局部和全局拓扑；3. 通过Q-Former将结构表示对齐到语言标记空间；4. 为掩码扩散专门数学重构分子结构偏好优化；5. 通过双向迭代去噪确保全局结构一致性

Result: MolDA在分子生成、描述和性质预测任务中展现出全局结构一致性、化学有效性和鲁棒推理能力

Conclusion: MolDA通过掩码扩散模型克服了自回归方法的局限性，为分子发现提供了更有效的多模态框架，能够更好地处理化学分子的全局约束和结构一致性要求

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced molecular discovery, but existing multimodal molecular architectures fundamentally rely on autoregressive (AR) backbones. This strict left-to-right inductive bias is sub-optimal for generating chemically valid molecules, as it struggles to account for non-local global constraints (e.g., ring closures) and often accumulates structural errors during sequential generation. To address these limitations, we propose MolDA (Molecular language model with masked Diffusion with mAsking), a novel multimodal framework that replaces the conventional AR backbone with a discrete Large Language Diffusion Model. MolDA extracts comprehensive structural representations using a hybrid graph encoder, which captures both local and global topologies, and aligns them into the language token space via a Q-Former. Furthermore, we mathematically reformulate Molecular Structure Preference Optimization specifically for the masked diffusion. Through bidirectional iterative denoising, MolDA ensures global structural coherence, chemical validity, and robust reasoning across molecule generation, captioning, and property prediction.

</details>


### [122] [Empirical Characterization of Rationale Stability Under Controlled Perturbations for Explainable Pattern Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04456)
*Abu Noman Md Sakib,Zhensen Wang,Merjulah Roby,Zijie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种评估模型解释一致性的新指标，通过计算相同标签输入的SHAP值余弦相似度来量化解释稳定性，并在SST-2数据集上验证其识别模型不一致行为的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI评估大多停留在实例层面，缺乏对相似输入间解释一致性的量化评估。可靠模式识别系统应在相似输入上表现一致，其解释也应保持稳定，这对构建可信AI系统至关重要。

Method: 提出基于SHAP特征重要性的一致性度量指标，计算相同标签输入的SHAP值余弦相似度。在SST-2情感分析数据集上使用预训练BERT模型实现，并在RoBERTa、DistilBERT和IMDB数据集上进行鲁棒性测试。

Result: 通过一系列实验验证了该指标识别模型预测错位和解释不一致的能力。与标准保真度指标对比表明，新指标能有效检测模型行为偏离预期目标的情况。

Conclusion: 该框架通过量化模型在相似输入上是否依赖一致的特征归因模式，为模型行为提供了更深入理解，支持在实际模式识别流程中进行更鲁棒的模型评估，有助于构建可信AI系统。

Abstract: Reliable pattern recognition systems should exhibit consistent behavior across similar inputs, and their explanations should remain stable. However, most Explainable AI evaluations remain instance centric and do not explicitly quantify whether attribution patterns are consistent across samples that share the same class or represent small variations of the same input. In this work, we propose a novel metric aimed at assessing the consistency of model explanations, ensuring that models consistently reflect the intended objectives and consistency under label-preserving perturbations. We implement this metric using a pre-trained BERT model on the SST-2 sentiment analysis dataset, with additional robustness tests on RoBERTa, DistilBERT, and IMDB, applying SHAP to compute feature importance for various test samples. The proposed metric quantifies the cosine similarity of SHAP values for inputs with the same label, aiming to detect inconsistent behaviors, such as biased reliance on certain features or failure to maintain consistent reasoning for similar predictions. Through a series of experiments, we evaluate the ability of this metric to identify misaligned predictions and inconsistencies in model explanations. These experiments are compared against standard fidelity metrics to assess whether the new metric can effectively identify when a model's behavior deviates from its intended objectives. The proposed framework provides a deeper understanding of model behavior by enabling more robust verification of rationale stability, which is critical for building trustworthy AI systems. By quantifying whether models rely on consistent attribution patterns for similar inputs, the proposed approach supports more robust evaluation of model behavior in practical pattern recognition pipelines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/anmspro/ESS-XAI-Stability.

</details>


### [123] [The Topology of Multimodal Fusion: Why Current Architectures Fail at Creative Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04465)
*Xiujiang Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文识别出现有多模态AI架构中的拓扑结构限制，提出基于中国工艺认识论的"象"概念和十字框架，通过哲学、认知科学和数学三支柱构建新理论，并设计实验验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态AI架构（如CLIP、GPT-4V/Gemini、扩散模型）存在共同的几何先验——模态可分性（接触拓扑），这限制了模型处理模态间深层交互的能力。作者旨在突破这一结构限制，从跨文明视角构建更强大的多模态理解框架。

Method: 1. 哲学支柱：重新诠释维特根斯坦的"说/显"区分，引入中国工艺认识论的"象"概念作为第三状态，构建十字框架（道/器×说/显）；2. 认知科学支柱：通过病理镜像重新解释DMN/ECN/SN三部分共激活，在二维参数空间中分析重叠同构与叠加崩溃；3. 数学支柱：使用纤维丛和杨-米尔斯曲率形式化这些概念；4. 提出UOO实现（神经ODE+拓扑正则化）、ANALOGY-MM基准（错误类型比度量）和META-TOP三层基准测试跨文明拓扑同构。

Result: 论文提出了完整的理论框架和实验方案，但未报告具体实验结果。主要成果包括：1. 识别出多模态AI的拓扑结构限制；2. 构建了基于跨文明哲学的理论框架；3. 设计了三个实验基准和验证路线图；4. 提出了明确的终止标准以确保可证伪性。

Conclusion: 当前多模态AI的模态可分性先验构成根本性限制，需要拓扑层面的突破而非参数优化。通过整合中国工艺认识论的"象"概念和现代数学工具，可以构建更强大的多模态理解框架。提出的实验方案为验证该理论提供了系统方法，具有明确的证伪机制。

Abstract: This paper identifies a structural limitation in current multimodal AI architectures that is topological rather than parametric. Contrastive alignment (CLIP), cross-attention fusion (GPT-4V/Gemini), and diffusion-based generation share a common geometric prior -- modal separability -- which we term contact topology. The argument rests on three pillars with philosophy as the generative center. The philosophical pillar reinterprets Wittgenstein's saying/showing distinction as a problem rather than a conclusion: where Wittgenstein chose silence, the Chinese craft epistemology tradition responded with xiang (operative schema) -- the third state emerging when saying and showing interpenetrate. A cruciform framework (dao/qi x saying/showing) positions xiang at the intersection, executing dual huacai (transformation-and-cutting) along both axes. This generates a dual-layer dynamics: chuanghua (creative transformation as spontaneous event) and huacai (its institutionalization into repeatable form). The cognitive science pillar reinterprets DMN/ECN/SN tripartite co-activation through the pathological mirror: overlap isomorphism vs. superimposition collapse in a 2D parameter space (coupling intensity x regulatory capacity). The mathematical pillar formalizes these via fiber bundles and Yang-Mills curvature, with the cruciform structure mapped to fiber bundle language. We propose UOO implementation via Neural ODEs with topological regularization, the ANALOGY-MM benchmark with error-type-ratio metric, and the META-TOP three-tier benchmark testing cross-civilizational topological isomorphism across seven archetypes. A phased experimental roadmap with explicit termination criteria ensures clean exit if falsified.

</details>


### [124] [What Makes a Sale? Rethinking End-to-End Seller--Buyer Retail Dynamics with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04468)
*Jeonghwan Choi,Jibin Hwang,Gyeonghun Sun,Minjeong Ban,Taewon Yun,Hyeonjae Cheon,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RetailSim是一个端到端的零售模拟框架，统一建模从卖家说服到买家决策的完整零售流程，通过双协议评估验证其行为保真度和经济规律再现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零售模拟器仅捕捉零售流程的部分方面，缺乏跨阶段依赖建模，难以评估早期决策如何影响下游结果，限制了零售策略部署前的有效评估。

Method: 提出RetailSim框架，在统一环境中建模完整零售管道，通过多样化产品空间、角色驱动的智能体和多轮交互实现模拟保真度。采用双协议评估：人类评估行为保真度和基于真实经济规律的元评估。

Result: RetailSim成功再现了关键经济模式，包括人口统计购买行为、价格-需求关系以及异质价格弹性。在角色推断、买卖双方交互分析和销售策略评估等实际用例中展示了实用价值。

Conclusion: RetailSim作为一个可控测试平台，具有探索零售策略的潜力，能够评估跨阶段依赖关系，为零售策略部署前的评估提供了有效的端到端模拟环境。

Abstract: Evaluating retail strategies before deployment is difficult, as outcomes are determined across multiple stages, from seller-side persuasion through buyer-seller interaction to purchase decisions. However, existing retail simulators capture only partial aspects of this process and do not model cross-stage dependencies, making it difficult to assess how early decisions affect downstream outcomes. We present RetailSim, an end-to-end retail simulation framework that models this pipeline in a unified environment, explicitly designed for simulation fidelity through diverse product spaces, persona-driven agents, and multi-turn interactions. We evaluate RetailSim with a dual protocol comprising human evaluation of behavioral fidelity and meta-evaluation against real-world economic regularities, showing that it successfully reproduces key patterns such as demographic purchasing behavior, the price-demand relationship, and heterogeneous price elasticity. We further demonstrate its practical utility via decision-oriented use cases, including persona inference, seller-buyer interaction analysis, and sales strategy evaluation, showing RetailSim's potential as a controlled testbed for exploring retail strategies.

</details>


### [125] [On the "Causality" Step in Policy Gradient Derivations: A Pedagogical Reconciliation of Full Return and Reward-to-Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04686)
*Nima H. Siboni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对策略梯度中REINFORCE估计器的推导进行了严格数学分析，澄清了"因果性"如何导致完整回报被奖励到当前时刻替代，通过前缀轨迹分布和得分函数恒等式给出了明确推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略梯度的入门介绍中，通常使用完整轨迹回报推导REINFORCE估计器，然后基于"因果性"说明完整回报可以被"奖励到当前时刻"替代。虽然这一说法正确，但通常的推导在严谨性上不足，未能清晰展示过去奖励项如何消失。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，提供更严格的数学推导。

Method: 采用前缀轨迹分布和得分函数恒等式的方法，将目标函数分解为前缀轨迹的期望，从而直接推导出奖励到当前时刻的形式。这种方法避免了传统推导中"因果性"作为额外启发式原则的使用。

Result: 推导出的估计器与传统REINFORCE估计器在形式上相同，但提供了更严格的数学基础。主要贡献是概念性的：展示了奖励到当前时刻如何直接从目标函数的前缀分解中自然产生，而非作为完整回报的事后无偏替代。

Conclusion: 本文通过严格的数学推导澄清了策略梯度中奖励到当前时刻的来源，将传统的"因果性"论证恢复为推导的自然推论而非额外启发式原则。这一分析增强了策略梯度理论基础的严谨性，为教学和理解提供了更清晰的框架。

Abstract: In introductory presentations of policy gradients, one often derives the REINFORCE estimator using the full trajectory return and then states, by ``causality,'' that the full return may be replaced by the reward-to-go. Although this statement is correct, it is frequently presented at a level of rigor that leaves unclear where the past-reward terms disappear. This short paper isolates that step and gives a mathematically explicit derivation based on prefix trajectory distributions and the score-function identity. The resulting account does not change the estimator. Its contribution is conceptual: instead of presenting reward-to-go as a post hoc unbiased replacement for full return, it shows that reward-to-go arises directly once the objective is decomposed over prefix trajectories. In this formulation, the usual causality argument is recovered as a corollary of the derivation rather than as an additional heuristic principle.

</details>


### [126] [AI Assistance Reduces Persistence and Hurts Independent Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04721)
*Grace Liu,Brian Christian,Tsvetomira Dumbalska,Michiel A. Bakker,Rachit Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI助手虽能短期提升任务表现，但会降低用户坚持性和无协助时的表现，即使短暂交互也会产生这些负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统被优化为提供即时完整的回答，这与人类导师注重长期能力培养的协作方式形成对比。研究旨在探究这种短视协作方式对人类学习行为的因果影响。

Method: 通过随机对照试验（N=1,222）研究人机交互，在数学推理和阅读理解等多种任务中，比较有AI协助和无AI协助条件下的表现差异，测量坚持性和无协助表现。

Result: 尽管AI协助在短期内提升表现，但人们在没有AI协助时表现显著更差，更容易放弃。这些效应在仅约10分钟的短暂AI交互后就会出现。

Conclusion: AI系统应优先考虑长期能力培养的脚手架支持，而不仅仅是即时任务完成。坚持性是技能习得的基础，AI设计需要平衡即时帮助与长期学习效果。

Abstract: People often optimize for long-term goals in collaboration: A mentor or companion doesn't just answer questions, but also scaffolds learning, tracks progress, and prioritizes the other person's growth over immediate results. In contrast, current AI systems are fundamentally short-sighted collaborators - optimized for providing instant and complete responses, without ever saying no (unless for safety reasons). What are the consequences of this dynamic? Here, through a series of randomized controlled trials on human-AI interactions (N = 1,222), we provide causal evidence for two key consequences of AI assistance: reduced persistence and impairment of unassisted performance. Across a variety of tasks, including mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension, we find that although AI assistance improves performance in the short-term, people perform significantly worse without AI and are more likely to give up. Notably, these effects emerge after only brief interactions with AI (approximately 10 minutes). These findings are particularly concerning because persistence is foundational to skill acquisition and is one of the strongest predictors of long-term learning. We posit that persistence is reduced because AI conditions people to expect immediate answers, thereby denying them the experience of working through challenges on their own. These results suggest the need for AI model development to prioritize scaffolding long-term competence alongside immediate task completion.

</details>


### [127] [Incompleteness of AI Safety Verification via Kolmogorov Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04876)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文证明了AI安全验证存在根本性信息理论限制：任何有限的形式验证器都无法认证所有高复杂度的策略合规实例


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在安全关键领域需要满足形式化安全策略约束，但现有验证方法存在局限性。作者旨在探究这些限制是否源于内在的信息理论边界，而非仅仅是计算复杂性或模型表达能力问题。

Method: 将策略合规形式化为编码系统行为的验证问题，使用柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂度进行分析。证明了一个不完备性结果：对于任何固定的可计算枚举的可靠验证器，存在一个阈值，超过该阈值后，复杂度高于该阈值的真实策略合规实例无法被认证。

Result: 证明了AI安全验证存在根本性信息理论限制：任何有限的形式验证器都无法认证所有策略合规实例，特别是当实例复杂度超过某个阈值时。这一限制独立于计算资源，揭示了验证的固有局限性。

Conclusion: AI安全验证存在内在的信息理论限制，这独立于计算复杂性。这一发现支持采用携带证明的方法，为实例级正确性提供保证，而不是试图构建能够认证所有可能实例的通用验证器。

Abstract: Ensuring that artificial intelligence (AI) systems satisfy formal safety and policy constraints is a central challenge in safety-critical domains. While limitations of verification are often attributed to combinatorial complexity and model expressiveness, we show that they arise from intrinsic information-theoretic limits. We formalize policy compliance as a verification problem over encoded system behaviors and analyze it using Kolmogorov complexity. We prove an incompleteness result: for any fixed sound computably enumerable verifier, there exists a threshold beyond which true policy-compliant instances cannot be certified once their complexity exceeds that threshold. Consequently, no finite formal verifier can certify all policy-compliant instances of arbitrarily high complexity. This reveals a fundamental limitation of AI safety verification independent of computational resources, and motivates proof-carrying approaches that provide instance-level correctness guarantees.

</details>


### [128] [Learning, Potential, and Retention: An Approach for Evaluating Adaptive AI-Enabled Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04878)
*Alexis Burgon,Berkman Sahiner,Nicholas A Petrick,Gene Pennello,Ravi K Samala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出评估医疗设备自适应AI模型的新方法，包含学习、潜力和保留三个互补测量指标，用于区分模型适应与动态环境变化导致的性能变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备自适应AI模型面临评估挑战，因为模型和评估数据集都会迭代更新，使得性能评估变得复杂。需要区分性能变化是由模型适应还是动态环境变化引起的。

Method: 提出包含三个互补测量指标的新方法：学习（模型在当前数据上的改进）、潜力（数据集驱动的性能变化）、保留（跨修改步骤的知识保存）。通过模拟人口分布变化进行案例研究。

Result: 案例研究表明：渐进式转变可实现稳定的学习和保留，而快速转变则揭示可塑性与稳定性之间的权衡。这些测量为监管科学提供实用见解。

Conclusion: 该方法能够严格评估自适应AI系统在连续修改过程中的安全性和有效性，为医疗设备自适应AI模型的监管评估提供实用框架。

Abstract: This work addresses challenges in evaluating adaptive artificial intelligence (AI) models for medical devices, where iterative updates to both models and evaluation datasets complicate performance assessment. We introduce a novel approach with three complementary measurements: learning (model improvement on current data), potential (dataset-driven performance shifts), and retention (knowledge preservation across modification steps), to disentangle performance changes caused by model adaptations versus dynamic environments. Case studies using simulated population shifts demonstrate the approach's utility: gradual transitions enable stable learning and retention, while rapid shifts reveal trade-offs between plasticity and stability. These measurements provide practical insights for regulatory science, enabling rigorous assessment of the safety and effectiveness of adaptive AI systems over sequential modifications.

</details>


### [129] [QED-Nano: Teaching a Tiny Model to Prove Hard Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04898)
*LM-Provers,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Jasper Dekoninck,Edward Beeching,Jia Li,Ian Wu,Lewis Tunstall,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QED-Nano是一个4B参数的开放模型，通过三阶段训练方法在奥林匹克数学证明问题上达到接近专有模型的性能，同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前专有AI系统在复杂数学证明问题上表现出色，但训练流程不透明、依赖大型内部模型和框架，导致运行成本高、难以复现、研究和改进。研究目标是探索小型开放模型是否也能在奥林匹克级别数学问题上达到有竞争力的推理性能。

Method: 采用三阶段训练方法：1) 监督微调，从DeepSeek-Math-V2蒸馏学习良好证明写作风格；2) 基于评分标准的强化学习；3) 扩展强化学习，引入推理缓存机制，将长证明分解为迭代的总结-精炼循环，增强测试时推理能力。

Result: QED-Nano在证明生成性能上超越了包括Nomos-1和GPT-OSS-120B在内的更大规模开放模型，接近Gemini 3 Pro等专有模型的性能，同时推理成本仅为后者的一小部分。

Conclusion: 小型开放模型通过精心设计的训练方法可以在奥林匹克级别数学证明问题上达到接近专有模型的性能，为开放数学推理研究提供了可行的解决方案。作者发布了完整的QED-Nano流程，包括模型、数据集和代码，以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Proprietary AI systems have recently demonstrated impressive capabilities on complex proof-based problems, with gold-level performance reported at the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO). However, the training pipelines behind these systems remain largely undisclosed, and their reliance on large "internal" models and scaffolds makes them expensive to run, difficult to reproduce, and hard to study or improve upon. This raises a central question: can small, open models also be trained to achieve competitive reasoning performance on difficult Olympiad-level math? In this paper, we answer this question by building QED-Nano, a 4B model post-trained for Olympiad-level proofs. Our training recipe has three stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning to imbue good proof-writing styles by distilling from DeepSeek-Math-V2, (2) reinforcement learning (RL) with rubric-based rewards, and (3) expanding RL with a reasoning cache, which decomposes long proofs into iterative summarize-and-refine cycles and enables stronger test-time reasoning. QED-Nano surpasses the proof-generation performance of much larger open models, including Nomos-1 and GPT-OSS-120B, and approaches the performance of proprietary models like Gemini 3 Pro, at a fraction of the inference cost. To support further research on open mathematical reasoning, we release the full QED-Nano pipeline, including the QED-Nano and QED-Nano-SFT models, the FineProofs-SFT and FineProofs-RL datasets, and the training and evaluation code.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Design and Implementation of an Open-Source Security Framework for Cloud Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03331)
*Wanru Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 开源云基础设施安全框架，通过微服务架构统一身份访问控制、配置基线检查、实时威胁监控和自动化修复，显著提升云安全防护效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业云环境的主要安全漏洞源于配置错误、权限过大和工具碎片化。现有工具分散，需要分别监控Kubernetes、OpenStack和基础设施即代码，缺乏统一的安全防护框架。

Method: 采用微服务架构构建开源云基础设施安全框架，集成四个核心服务：1)身份和访问控制统一；2)基于智能检查的Kubernetes和OpenStack配置基线验证；3)基于Falco运行时规则和ELK分析的实时威胁监控；4)基于Terraform计划和Checkov/OPA策略结果的自动化修复。

Result: 在企业级测试环境中，漏洞评估时间从基准工具链的120分钟减少到18分钟，误报率低于5%。持续部署后，可观测安全事件数量下降62%。项目采用Apache 2.0许可，为中小团队降低约40%的入门成本。

Conclusion: 该框架通过统一的安全防护方法有效解决了云环境中的配置和身份安全问题，显著提升了安全运维效率，降低了中小团队采用云安全技术的门槛。

Abstract: Misconfiguration, excessive privilege, and tool fragmentation remain the main reasons why enterprise cloud environments are breached. Recent reports on cloud-native application protection note that most incidents can be traced back to configuration or identity errors rather than platform flaws, and that organizations still need separate tools to watch Kubernetes, OpenStack, and infrastructure-as-code. To address this gap, this paper presents an open-source cloud-infrastructure security framework built with a microservice architecture. The framework integrates four core services: 1) identity and access control unification, 2) configuration-baseline intelligent checking over Kubernetes and OpenStack assets, 3) real-time threat monitoring based on Falco-style runtime rules and ELK-based analytics, and 4) automated remediation that consumes Terraform plans and Checkov/OPA policy results to roll back or harden resources. It provides automated deployment, supports 50-200-node clusters, and exposes uniform REST and gRPC interfaces for extension. In an enterprise-grade testbed, vulnerability-assessment time was reduced from 120 min as baseline toolchain to 18 min, with false-positive rate below 5%. After continuous deployment, the number of observable security events dropped by 62%. The project is released under Apache 2.0 to lower entry cost by about 40% for small and medium teams.

</details>


### [131] [Security Analysis of Universal Circuits as a Mechanism for Hardware Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03396)
*Zain Ul Abideen,Deepali Garg,Lawrence Pileggi,Samuel Pagliarini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估了通用电路（UCs）在硬件IP混淆中的安全性，测试了针对最先进的有指导和无指导攻击的防御能力，结果显示UCs能有效保护IP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用电路（UCs）为硬件知识产权（IP）混淆提供了一种有前景的方法，利用密码学原理在可编程逻辑结构中隐藏结构和功能。其适应性使其特别适合全球化的集成电路（IC）供应链，其中防范逆向工程等威胁的安全性至关重要。尽管潜力巨大，但UC的安全性在很大程度上尚未被探索。

Method: 该工作评估了UCs针对最先进的有指导攻击（oracle-guided, OG）和无指导攻击（oracle-less, OL）的安全性。通过实验测试这两种攻击方法对UCs的破解效果。

Result: 结果显示，有指导攻击（OG）的成功率接近随机（约50%），而无指导攻击（OL）显示出最小的结构泄漏。这表明UCs能有效抵抗这两种类型的攻击。

Conclusion: 这些发现共同证实了通用电路（UCs）用于IP保护的可行性。UCs在硬件IP混淆中表现出强大的安全性，能够有效防范逆向工程等威胁。

Abstract: Universal Circuits (UCs) offer a promising approach to hardware Intellectual Property (IP) obfuscation, leveraging cryptographic principles to hide both structure and function in a programmable logic fabric. Their adaptability makes them especially suitable for the globalized Integrated Circuit (IC) supply chain, where security against threats like reverse engineering is crucial. Despite the potential, UC security remains largely unexplored. This work evaluates UC security against state-of-the-art oracle-guided (OG) and oracle-less (OL) attacks. Results show near-random success rates (approx 50%) for OG attacks whereas OL attacks display minimal structural leakage. Collectively, these findings confirm the feasibility of UCs for IP protection.

</details>


### [132] [CIPHR: Cryptography Inspired IP Protection through Fine-Grain Hardware Redaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03560)
*Aritra Dasgupta,Sudipta Paria,Swarup Bhunia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CIPHR是一种基于密码学不可区分性原理的细粒度硬件编辑方法，通过启发式随机化引入显著结构变换，提供对结构攻击的鲁棒保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球化集成电路供应链中硬件IP面临广泛的机密性和完整性攻击。现有IP保护方法（包括硬件编辑）往往留下可被攻击者利用的结构特征，类似于密码学中的已知明文攻击。

Method: 提出CIPHR方法，受密码学不可区分性启发，采用新颖的启发式驱动随机化技术，在编辑设计中引入显著的结构变换，实现细粒度硬件编辑。

Result: 使用结构分析指标评估CIPHR的安全性，与多种最先进IP保护技术对比。多个开源基准设计证明CIPHR的细粒度编辑具有鲁棒性、可扩展性，并能抵抗结构攻击。

Conclusion: CIPHR提供了一种基于不可区分性原则的硬件IP保护方法，能够有效防御利用结构特征发起的攻击，相比现有技术提供更强的安全性。

Abstract: Hardware intellectual property (IP) in the globalized integrated circuit (IC) supply chain is exposed to a wide range of confidentiality and integrity attacks by untrusted third-party entities. Existing IP-level countermeasures, such as logic locking, hardware obfuscation, camouflaging, and redaction, have aimed at addressing these them. In particular, hardware redaction has emerged as a robust approach for IP protection against confidentiality attacks, including reverse engineering. We note that existing IP protection approaches, including the ones based on hardware redaction, tend to leave behind structural artifacts that can be exploited by adversaries to bypass protections or predict unlocking keys, using the knowledge of known designs, akin to a known-plaintext attack (KPA) in cryptography. In this work, we present CIPHR, a robust fine-grain hardware redaction methodology inspired by the cryptographic property of indistinguishability. The proposed approach utilizes novel heuristic-driven randomization to introduce significant structural transformations into the redacted designs. We employ structural analysis metrics to evaluate the security achieved by CIPHR compared to various state-of-the-art IP protection techniques. Multiple open-source benchmark designs are used to demonstrate that fine-grain redaction in CIPHR is robust, scalable, and indistinguishable against structural attacks.

</details>


### [133] [SecPI: Secure Code Generation with Reasoning Models via Security Reasoning Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03587)
*Hao Wang,Niels Mündler,Mark Vero,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecPI是一个微调管道，通过让推理语言模型内化结构化安全推理，无需推理时安全指令即可生成安全代码


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型在代码生成中经常引入严重安全漏洞，而基于训练的方法依赖有限的手动安全数据集，推理级通用安全提示会降低功能正确性且只能触发浅层漏洞分析

Method: SecPI使用LLM分类器筛选通用编码数据集中的安全相关任务，通过教师模型生成高质量安全推理轨迹（系统枚举相关CWE和缓解措施），然后在无安全提示的输入和教师推理轨迹对上微调目标模型

Result: SecPI显著提升模型安全性能：QwQ 32B在CWEval上功能正确且安全的生成从48.2%提升至62.2%（+14.0点），在BaxBench上从18.2%提升至22.0%；展现出强大的跨CWE和跨语言泛化能力

Conclusion: SecPI通过结构化安全推理微调，使模型能够自主进行安全推理而非依赖显式指令，有效解决了推理语言模型在安全代码生成中的关键问题

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) are increasingly used in programming. Yet, even state-of-the-art RLMs frequently introduce critical security vulnerabilities in generated code. Prior training-based approaches for secure code generation face a critical limitation that prevents their direct application to RLMs: they rely on costly, manually curated security datasets covering only a limited set of vulnerabilities. At the inference level, generic security reminders consistently degrade functional correctness while triggering only shallow ad-hoc vulnerability analysis. To address these problems, we present SecPI, a fine-tuning pipeline that teaches RLMs to internalize structured security reasoning, producing secure code by default without any security instructions at inference time. SecPI filters existing general-purpose coding datasets for security-relevant tasks using an LLM-based classifier, generates high-quality security reasoning traces with a teacher model guided by a structured prompt that systematically enumerates relevant CWEs and mitigations, and fine-tunes the target model on pairs of inputs with no security prompt and teacher reasoning traces -- as a result, the model learns to reason about security autonomously rather than in response to explicit instructions. An extensive evaluation on security benchmarks with state-of-the-art open-weight reasoning models validates the effectiveness of our approach. For instance, SecPI improves the percentage of functionally correct and secure generations for QwQ 32B from 48.2% to 62.2% (+14.0 points) on CWEval and from 18.2% to 22.0% on BaxBench. Further investigation also reveals strong cross-CWE and cross-language generalization beyond training vulnerabilities. Even when trained only on injection-related CWEs, QwQ 32B generates correct and secure code 9.9% more frequently on held-out memory-safety CWEs.

</details>


### [134] [ProtoGuard-SL: Prototype Consistency Based Backdoor Defense for Vertical Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03595)
*Yuhan Shui,Ruobin Jin,Zhihao Dou,Zhiqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProtoGuard-SL：一种针对垂直分裂学习中毒攻击的服务器端防御方法，通过利用嵌入空间中的类条件表示一致性来检测和过滤异常嵌入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直分裂学习虽然能实现跨方协作训练而不共享原始数据，但近期研究表明其极易受到基于中毒的后门攻击。恶意客户端可在中间嵌入中注入隐蔽触发器，操纵服务器端模型且难以检测，现有防御方法对自适应攻击的鲁棒性有限。

Method: ProtoGuard-SL采用两阶段框架：1) 构建鲁棒的类原型；2) 将嵌入转换为原型一致性表示，然后采用类条件、无分布的保形过滤策略来识别和移除异常嵌入。该方法利用良性嵌入在同一类内表现出稳定语义对齐，而中毒嵌入会破坏这种结构的观察。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、SVHN和Bank Marketing三个数据集上，在三种不同攻击设置下的广泛实验表明，该方法实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: ProtoGuard-SL通过利用嵌入空间中的类条件表示一致性，有效提高了分裂学习对中毒攻击的鲁棒性，为服务器端防御提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vertical split learning (SL) enables collaborative model training across parties holding complementary features without sharing raw data, but recent work has shown that it is highly vulnerable to poisoning-based backdoor attacks operating on intermediate embeddings. By compromising malicious clients, adversaries can inject stealthy triggers that manipulate the server-side model while remaining difficult to detect, and existing defenses provide limited robustness against adaptive attacks. In this paper, we propose ProtoGuard-SL, a server-side defense that improves the robustness of split learning by exploiting class-conditional representation consistency in the embedding space. Our approach is motivated by the observation that benign embeddings within the same class exhibit stable semantic alignment, whereas poisoned embeddings inevitably disrupt this structure. ProtoGuard-SL adopts a two-stage framework that constructs robust class prototypes and transforms embeddings into a prototype-consistency representation, followed by a class-conditional, distribution-free conformal filtering strategy to identify and remove anomalous embeddings. Extensive experiments are conducted on three datasets, CIFAR-10, SVHN, and Bank Marketing, under three different attack settings demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [135] [AttackEval: A Systematic Empirical Study of Prompt Injection Attack Effectiveness Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03598)
*Jackson Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AttackEval系统评估提示注入攻击效果，发现混淆攻击成功率最高，语义/社交攻击对意图感知防御有效，复合攻击成功率可达97.6%，揭示了当前防御的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于提示注入的研究主要集中在防御方面，而攻击侧的有效策略和原因研究不足。需要系统性地评估不同提示注入攻击的有效性，以识别当前防御系统的盲点。

Method: 构建包含10个攻击类别的分类法（分为句法、上下文、语义/社交三大类），每类25个精心设计的提示（共250个），在模拟生产系统中评估四种渐进增强的防御层级。

Result: 混淆攻击对意图感知防御成功率最高（ASR=0.76）；语义/社交攻击对意图感知防御保持高成功率（0.44-0.48）；复合攻击显著提升成功率（OBF+EM达97.6%）；隐蔽性与语义防御下的剩余成功率正相关（r=0.71）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前防御系统的具体盲点，为设计更鲁棒的LLM安全系统提供了可操作的指导，未来防御需要同时优化结构和行为信号检测。

Abstract: Prompt injection has emerged as a critical vulnerability in large language model (LLM) deployments, yet existing research is heavily weighted toward defenses. The attack side -- specifically, which injection strategies are most effective and why -- remains insufficiently studied.We address this gap with AttackEval, a systematic empirical study of prompt injection attack effectiveness. We construct a taxonomy of ten attack categories organized into three parent groups (Syntactic, Contextual, and Semantic/Social), populate each category with 25 carefully crafted prompts (250 total), and evaluate them against a simulated production victim system under four progressively stronger defense tiers. Experiments reveal several non-obvious findings: (1) Obfuscation (OBF) achieves the highest single-attack success rate (ASR = 0.76) against even intent-aware defenses, because it defeats both keyword matching and semantic similarity checks simultaneously; (2) Semantic/Social attacks - Emotional Manipulation (EM) and Reward Framing (RF) - maintain high ASR (0.44-0.48) against intent-aware defenses due to their natural language surface, which evades structural anomaly detection; (3) Composite attacks combining two complementary strategies dramatically boost ASR, with the OBF + EM pair reaching 97.6%; (4) Stealth correlates positively with residual ASR against semantic defenses (r = 0.71), implying that future defenses must jointly optimize for both structural and behavioral signals. Our findings identify concrete blind spots in current defenses and provide actionable guidance for designing more robust LLM safety systems.

</details>


### [136] [Perceptual Gaps: ASCII Art and Overlapping Audio as CAPTCHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03612)
*Choon-Hou Rafael Chong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种新型CAPTCHA：基于视觉的ASCII艺术文本CAPTCHA和基于音频的问答任务CAPTCHA，利用人类进化出的专门神经处理能力来区分人类与AI模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大语言模型的发展，传统CAPTCHA已无法有效区分人类与机器人。研究旨在探索利用人类进化出的高度专门化神经处理能力的任务来创建更有效的CAPTCHA系统。

Method: 提出两类CAPTCHA：1) 视觉CAPTCHA：将字母数字字符串渲染为ASCII艺术；2) 音频CAPTCHA：基于重叠或噪声干扰的音频上下文的问答任务。使用前沿LLM（GPT 5.2、Gemini 3等）评估视觉CAPTCHA的文本和图像输入，通过背景噪声、高斯噪声和重叠语音等增强技术评估音频CAPTCHA。

Result: 所有LLM均无法解决任何ASCII艺术CAPTCHA，最佳模型最多只能推断1-2个字符。支持音频的模型在解决音频CAPTCHA时表现仅略优于随机猜测。这些CAPTCHA在当前极其有效，但能否抵御快速发展的AI技术尚不确定。

Conclusion: 提出的CAPTCHA在当前环境下非常有效，但需要进一步研究确定这些任务是AI的暂时弱点还是更持久的区分人类与机器人的方法。后续研究需评估其在快速演进的AI环境中的长期有效性。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (LLMs) advance, traditional CAPTCHAs have become obsolete at distinguishing humans from bots. To address this shift, this paper aims to investigate the possibility of using tasks for which humans have evolved highly specialised neural processing. We introduce two CAPTCHA classes: a vision-based CAPTCHA, which renders alphanumeric strings as ASCII art, and an audio-based CAPTCHA, which is a question-answering task with overlapping or noise-corrupted audio context. We evaluate our vision-based CAPTCHA both as text and image input with multiple frontier LLMs (GPT 5.2, Gemini 3, etc.), and assess our audio-based CAPTCHAs by applying augmentations like background noise, Gaussian noise, and overlapping speech. We determined that none of the LLMs were able to solve a single ASCII-based CAPTCHA, with the best performing model only being able to infer at most one or two characters. Additionally, all models that supported audio performed only modestly better than random when solving audio CAPTCHAs. Our results suggest that these CAPTCHAs are exceptionally effective today, but it is unclear whether it can withstand the fast-evolving landscape of artificial intelligence. Subsequent research is needed to determine whether these tasks are temporary vulnerabilities or represent a more durable method of distinguishing humans from bots.

</details>


### [137] [A Faceted Classification of Authenticator-Centric Authentication Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03627)
*Alex R. Mattukat,Vincent Schmandt,Timo Langstrof,Michael Zerbe,Horst Lichter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种新的面向认证技术和认证器的分面分类方案，并通过LLM辅助文献综述构建了认证技术和认证器目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认证技术分类方法在分类维度上有限，且不够灵活，无法适应认证技术的多样性。需要更全面、灵活的分类方案来系统化组织认证技术。

Method: 基于345篇文献进行有针对性的LLM辅助文献综述和语义聚类，开发了认证技术和认证器的分面分类方案，并应用这些方案构建了认证技术和认证器目录。

Result: 提出了两种新颖的分面分类方案，并构建了包含多种认证技术和认证器的系统化目录，为认证技术的研究和应用提供了结构化框架。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分面分类方案比现有方法更全面、灵活，能够更好地组织和理解多样化的认证技术，为未来认证技术研究提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Authentication is a fundamental security means for protecting system resources. Authenticator-centric authentication techniques (AuthN Techniques) address how mechanisms and credentials are used via Authenticators. There are many AuthN Techniques that differ in many ways and there exist classification approaches that aim to structure them. However, they are limited in the aspects they classify and are not flexible enough to accommodate the diverse nature of AuthN Techniques. This paper presents two contributions. First, novel, faceted classification schemes for AuthN Techniques and Authenticators are presented. The schemes were developed based on 345 papers identified through a targeted LLM-assisted literature review and semantic clustering. The classification schemes were applied to build a catalog of Authenticators and AuthN Techniques; the second contribution of this paper. This paper presents our methodology, the classification schemes with example applications, the list of AuthN Techniques from the catalog, and discussions on future work.

</details>


### [138] [Explainable PQC: A Layered Interpretive Framework for Post-Quantum Cryptographic Security Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03665)
*Daisuke Ishii,Rizwan Jahangir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个名为"可解释PQC"的分层框架，用于表示和传达后量子密码学的安全假设，该框架包含复杂性解释模型、数学结构分析和实验平台三个层次，但不替代形式化密码证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何通过结构化、分层的框架来更好地表示和传达后量子密码学的安全假设，使其既可用于技术解释，又不会替代形式化的密码证明。论文以基于格的PQC（包括ML-KEM和ML-DSA）作为案例研究。

Method: 提出了"可解释PQC"框架，包含三个层次：1）基于复杂性的解释模型，区分经典安全、量子安全和归约支持的硬度；2）应用组合霍奇理论和多面体几何探索格硬度结构方面的数学研究；3）在Julia中实现的实验平台，用于测量低维设置中格基约简算法的行为。

Result: 论文贡献主要是概念性和组织性的：定义了分层解释框架，阐明了其相对于形式化密码证明和基于归约的安全论证的范围，并确定了通过数学和实现层面方向更透明地传达PQC安全声明的途径。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一个用于理解和传达后量子密码安全假设的结构化框架，强调其作为解释工具的补充作用而非替代形式证明。框架通过复杂性理论、数学分析和实验验证三个层次提供了更透明的安全声明沟通方式，但明确不提供新的密码硬度结果、攻击方法或具体安全参数估计。

Abstract: This paper studies how post-quantum cryptographic (PQC) security assumptions can be represented and communicated through a structured, layered framework that is useful for technical interpretation but does not replace formal cryptographic proofs.
  We propose ``Explainable PQC,'' an interdisciplinary framework connecting three layers: (1) a complexity-based interpretive model that distinguishes classical security, quantum security, and reduction-backed hardness, drawing on computational complexity classes as supporting language; (2) an exploratory mathematical investigation applying combinatorial Hodge theory and polyhedral geometry to study structural aspects of lattice hardness; and (3)~an empirical experimentation platform, implemented in Julia, for measuring the behavior of lattice basis reduction algorithms (LLL, BKZ) in low-dimensional settings. The motivating case study throughout the paper is lattice-based PQC, including ML-KEM (FIPS 203) and ML-DSA (FIPS 204).
  The contribution of this paper is conceptual and organizational: it defines a layered interpretive framework, clarifies its scope relative to formal cryptographic proofs and reduction-based security arguments, and identifies mathematical and implementation-level directions through which PQC security claims may be more transparently communicated. This paper does not claim new cryptographic hardness results, new attacks, or concrete security parameter estimates.

</details>


### [139] [CREBench: Evaluating Large Language Models in Cryptographic Binary Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03750)
*Baicheng Chen,Yu Wang,Ziheng Zhou,Xiangru Liu,Juanru Li,Yilei Chen,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CREBench是一个用于评估大语言模型在密码学二进制逆向工程能力的基准测试，包含432个挑战，基于48种标准密码算法和3种不安全密钥使用场景，结果显示GPT-5.4得分64.03/100，人类专家基线为92.19。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向工程在软件安全中至关重要，特别是对于处理敏感数据的密码学程序。尽管大语言模型有潜力自动化这一过程，但其在逆向工程方面的能力尚未得到系统探索，特别是在密码学二进制逆向工程领域。

Method: 创建CREBench基准测试，包含432个挑战，基于48种标准密码算法、3种不安全密钥使用场景和3个难度级别。设计包含四个子任务的评估框架：算法识别、密钥恢复、输入恢复和正确标志恢复。评估了八个前沿大语言模型。

Result: GPT-5.4表现最佳，得分64.03/100，在59%的挑战中成功恢复标志。人类专家基线为92.19分，表明人类在密码学逆向工程任务中仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在密码学二进制逆向工程方面展现出一定能力，但仍远未达到人类专家水平。CREBench为评估和改进LLM在逆向工程能力提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Reverse engineering (RE) is central to software security, particularly for cryptographic programs that handle sensitive data and are highly prone to vulnerabilities. It supports critical tasks such as vulnerability discovery and malware analysis. Despite its importance, RE remains labor-intensive and requires substantial expertise, making large language models (LLMs) a potential solution for automating the process. However, their capabilities for RE remain systematically underexplored. To address this gap, we study the cryptographic binary RE capabilities of LLMs and introduce \textbf{CREBench}, a benchmark comprising 432 challenges built from 48 standard cryptographic algorithms, 3 insecure crypto key usage scenarios, and 3 difficulty levels. Each challenge follows a Capture-the-Flag (CTF) RE challenge, requiring the model to analyze the underlying cryptographic logic and recover the correct input. We design an evaluation framework comprising four sub-tasks, from algorithm identification to correct flag recovery. We evaluate eight frontier LLMs on CREBench. GPT-5.4, the best-performing model, achieves 64.03 out of 100 and recovers the flag in 59\% of challenges. We also establish a strong human expert baseline of 92.19 points, showing that humans maintain an advantage in cryptographic RE tasks. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/wangyu-ovo/CREBench.

</details>


### [140] [Partial Number Theoretic Transform Masking in Post Quantum Cryptography Hardware: A Security Margin Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03813)
*Ray Iskander,Khaled Kirah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对Adams Bridge硬件加速器的侧信道安全评估：该设计部分屏蔽INTT层并依赖洗牌，但实际安全边际低于设计者估计，通过多种分析方法揭示了攻击模型差距和实际脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Adams Bridge硬件加速器对ML-DSA和ML-KEM的侧信道安全声明，该设计声称通过部分屏蔽INTT层和洗牌提供高安全边际（ML-DSA 2^46，ML-KEM 2^96），但需要验证这些声明是否与已发表的侧信道文献一致。

Method: 采用七种分析路径的综合评估方法：1) RTL分析验证洗牌熵值；2) 软分析攻击管道量化攻击模型差距；3) 完整INTT因子图上的信念传播分析；4) 信息论界限分析；5) 层消融分析识别收敛条件；6) 观测拓扑分析；7) 提出战略性屏蔽对策。

Result: RTL分析显示随机起始索引洗牌仅提供6比特熵（64种排序），而非设计假设的296比特完全随机排列。软分析攻击实现37比特枚举缩减。信念传播在完整INTT上实现100%系数恢复。信息论界限显示SNRxN低至15时观测已包含足够互信息。观测拓扑而非数量决定恢复成功率：4个均匀分布层实现100%恢复，而4个连续层为0%。战略性屏蔽3个连续中间层（43%开销）可抵御软分析攻击。

Conclusion: Adams Bridge的安全边际被高估，其部分屏蔽设计存在实际脆弱性。研究提出了结合RTL验证、认知置信度标记、敏感性场景分析和实验验证的可重用安全边际审计方法，适用于任何部分屏蔽的NTT加速器。观测拓扑是决定侧信道恢复的关键因素，战略性层屏蔽可提供有效的安全对策。

Abstract: Adams Bridge, a hardware accelerator for ML-DSA and ML-KEM designed for the Caliptra root of trust, masks 1 of its Inverse Number Theoretic Transform (INTT) layers and relies on shuffling for the remainder, claiming per-butterfly Correlation Power Analysis (CPA) complexities of 2^46 (ML-DSA) and 2^96 (ML-KEM). We evaluate these claims against published side-channel literature across seven analysis tracks with confidence-rated evidence. Register-Transfer Level (RTL) analysis confirms that the design's Random Start Index (RSI) shuffling provides 6 bits of entropy per layer (64 orderings) rather than the 296 bits of a full random permutation assumed in its scaling argument, with effective margins below the designers' estimates. A soft-analytical attack pipeline demonstrates a 37-bit enumeration reduction, independent of Belief Propagation (BP) gains, quantifying the attack-model gap without achieving key recovery. Full-scale BP on the complete INTT factor graph achieves 100% coefficient recovery over the single-layer baseline, resolving whether BP gains scale to production-size Number Theoretic Transform (NTT) structures. A genie-aided information-theoretic bound shows observations contain sufficient mutual information for full recovery at SNRxN as low as 15. Layer-ablation analysis identifies four necessary conditions governing BP convergence. Observation topology, not count, determines recovery: 4 evenly spread layers achieve 100% while 4 consecutive layers achieve 0%, yielding a practical countermeasure design tool. Strategic masking of 3 consecutive mid-layers (43% overhead vs. full masking) creates an unrecoverable gap that defeats soft-analytical attacks. We contribute a reusable security margin audit methodology combining RTL verification, epistemic confidence tagging, sensitivity-scenario analysis, and experimental validation applicable to any partially masked NTT accelerator.

</details>


### [141] [Explainability-Guided Adversarial Attacks on Transformer-Based Malware Detectors Using Control Flow Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03843)
*Andrew Wheeler,Kshitiz Aryal,Maanak Gupta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对基于Transformer的恶意软件检测系统，提出了一种利用可解释性机制的白盒对抗攻击方法，通过替换CFG中关键函数调用来实现逃避检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的恶意软件检测系统在CFG图模态上表现良好，但其对抗逃避攻击的鲁棒性尚未充分研究。特别是将CFG线性化为函数调用序列的设计选择可能引入令牌级敏感性和排序伪影，这些可能被攻击者利用

Method: 提出白盒对抗逃避攻击，利用集成梯度等可解释性机制识别CFG中最具影响力的图组件。通过令牌级和词级归因，迭代地将正向归因的函数调用替换为合成的外部导入，生成对抗性CFG表示而不改变整体程序结构

Result: 在小型和大型Windows PE数据集上的实验评估表明，该方法能可靠地诱导误分类，即使针对训练到高精度的模型。攻击成功率显著，揭示了基于Transformer的恶意软件检测器的脆弱性

Conclusion: 可解释性工具虽然对模型可解释性有价值，但也可能暴露基于Transformer的恶意软件检测器的关键攻击面。将CFG线性化为序列的设计选择引入了可被利用的漏洞，需要在鲁棒性方面进一步研究

Abstract: Transformer-based malware detection systems operating on graph modalities such as control flow graphs (CFGs) achieve strong performance by modeling structural relationships in program behavior. However, their robustness to adversarial evasion attacks remains underexplored. This paper examines the vulnerability of a RoBERTa-based malware detector that linearizes CFGs into sequences of function calls, a design choice that enables transformer modeling but may introduce token-level sensitivities and ordering artifacts exploitable by adversaries. By evaluating evasion strategies within this graph-to-sequence framework, we provide insight into the practical robustness of transformer-based malware detectors beyond aggregate detection accuracy.
  This paper proposes a white-box adversarial evasion attack that leverages explainability mechanisms to identify and perturb most influential graph components. Using token- and word-level attributions derived from integrated gradients, the attack iteratively replaces positively attributed function calls with synthetic external imports, producing adversarial CFG representations without altering overall program structure. Experimental evaluation on small- and large-scale Windows Portable Executable (PE) datasets demonstrates that the proposed method can reliably induce misclassification, even against models trained to high accuracy. Our results highlight that explainability tools, while valuable for interpretability, can also expose critical attack surfaces in transformer-based malware detectors.

</details>


### [142] [Defending Buffer Overflows in WebAssembly: A Transpiler Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03859)
*Weiqi Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种WebAssembly转译器，通过添加栈金丝雀和地址空间布局随机化来防止缓冲区溢出漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly作为流行编译目标，源语言漏洞会转移到二进制文件中，需要保护未管理内存中的缓冲区溢出

Method: 开发WebAssembly转译器，接受WebAssembly二进制文件并添加栈金丝雀和地址空间布局随机化(ASLR)机制

Result: 实现了能够防止缓冲区溢出的WebAssembly转译器，增强了WebAssembly运行时的内存安全性

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效保护WebAssembly二进制文件免受缓冲区溢出攻击，提高了WebAssembly运行时的安全性

Abstract: WebAssembly is quickly becoming a popular compilation target for a variety of code. However, vulnerabilities in the source languages translate to vulnerabilities in the WebAssembly binaries. This work proposes a methodology and a WebAssembly transpiler to prevent buffer overflows in the unmanaged memory of the WebAssembly runtime. The transpiler accepts a WebAssembly binary and adds stack canaries and Address Space Layout Randomization (ASLR) to protect against buffer overflows.

</details>


### [143] [SecureAFL: Secure Asynchronous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03862)
*Anjun Gao,Feng Wang,Zhenglin Wan,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecureAFL：一种针对异步联邦学习中毒攻击的安全框架，通过异常更新检测和缺失客户端贡献估计来增强鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步联邦学习虽然解决了同步FL中的掉队者问题，但其去中心化特性使其容易受到中毒攻击。现有的异步FL防御机制要么对高级攻击无效，要么依赖于不现实的服务器假设。

Method: SecureAFL框架包含两个核心组件：1）异常更新检测与丢弃机制；2）缺失客户端贡献估计。同时采用拜占庭鲁棒聚合技术（如坐标中位数）来整合接收到的更新和估计的更新。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明了SecureAFL的有效性，能够有效防御异步联邦学习中的中毒攻击。

Conclusion: SecureAFL为异步联邦学习提供了一个有效的安全框架，通过结合异常检测、贡献估计和拜占庭鲁棒聚合，显著提升了系统对中毒攻击的防御能力。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train a global machine learning model via a server without sharing their private training data. In traditional FL, the system follows a synchronous approach, where the server waits for model updates from numerous clients before aggregating them to update the global model. However, synchronous FL is hindered by the straggler problem. To address this, the asynchronous FL architecture allows the server to update the global model immediately upon receiving any client's local model update. Despite its advantages, the decentralized nature of asynchronous FL makes it vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Several defenses tailored for asynchronous FL have been proposed, but these mechanisms remain susceptible to advanced attacks or rely on unrealistic server assumptions. In this paper, we introduce SecureAFL, an innovative framework designed to secure asynchronous FL against poisoning attacks. SecureAFL improves the robustness of asynchronous FL by detecting and discarding anomalous updates while estimating the contributions of missing clients. Additionally, it utilizes Byzantine-robust aggregation techniques, such as coordinate-wise median, to integrate the received and estimated updates. Extensive experiments on various real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SecureAFL.

</details>


### [144] [Graduated Trust Gating for IoT Location Verification: Trading Off Detection and Proof Escalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03896)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于物联网位置服务的渐进式信任门控机制，通过多信号完整性评分将位置验证映射到三种操作（通过、升级验证、拒绝），并采用会话锁定机制防止分数恢复，相比传统二元检测器显著降低了误拒率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网位置服务通常直接接受客户端报告的GPS坐标，但使用消费级工具进行欺骗很容易。现有的欺骗检测器输出二元决策，迫使系统设计者必须在高误拒率和高误接受率之间做出选择，缺乏灵活的中间验证选项。

Method: 提出渐进式信任门控机制：1）计算多信号完整性评分；2）将评分映射到三种操作：PROCEED（通过）、STEP-UP（升级验证，如零知识邻近证明）、DENY（拒绝）；3）引入会话锁定机制，确保单个可疑定位点会阻止整个会话，防止分数恢复后通过验证。

Result: 在10,000条合成轨迹的理想化升级验证条件下，渐进式门控能够使用严格阈值（θ_p=0.9），而二元门控无法安全使用：在匹配的误接受率（11%）下，渐进式门控保持零误拒率，而二元门控为0.05%，评分开销仅5微秒。真实设备轨迹显示，附近模拟位置（约550米）在θ_p=0.7时能通过，但在θ_p=0.9时会被路由到升级验证。信号消融分析确定了适合资源受限评分层的最小双信号配置（F1=0.84）。

Conclusion: 渐进式信任门控机制通过引入中间验证步骤和会话锁定，为物联网位置服务提供了更灵活、更安全的位置验证方案，显著优于传统的二元欺骗检测器，在保持低误接受率的同时实现了零误拒率。

Abstract: IoT location services accept client-reported GPS coordinates at face value, yet spoofing is trivial with consumer-grade tools. Existing spoofing detectors output a binary decision, forcing system designers to choose between high false-deny and high false-accept rates. We propose a graduated trust gate that computes a multi-signal integrity score and maps it to three actions: PROCEED, STEP-UP, or DENY, where STEP-UP invokes a stronger verifier such as a zero-knowledge proximity proof. A session-latch mechanism ensures that a single suspicious fix blocks the entire session, preventing post-transition score recovery. Under an idealized step-up oracle on 10,000 synthetic traces, the gate enables strict thresholds (theta_p = 0.9) that a binary gate cannot safely use: at matched false-accept rate (11%), the graduated gate maintains zero false-deny rate versus 0.05% for binary, with 5 microseconds scoring overhead. Real-device traces from an Android smartphone demonstrate the session-latch mechanism and show that a nearby mock location (~550 m) evades theta_p = 0.7 but is routed to step-up at theta_p = 0.9. Signal ablation identifies a minimal two-signal configuration (F1 = 0.84) suitable for resource-constrained scoring layers.

</details>


### [145] [Context-Binding Gaps in Stateful Zero-Knowledge Proximity Proofs: Taxonomy, Separation, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03900)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文分析了零知识邻近证明在状态化地理内容系统中的上下文绑定漏洞，提出了Zairn-ZKP方案，通过将身份、策略版本和会话上下文嵌入电路输入来减少操作不变量，相比离线验证方案降低了假设面


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识邻近证明仅证明几何邻近性，但不承诺应用上下文。在状态化地理内容系统中，由于投放点可共享坐标、策略演进、内容具有持久身份，这种间隙可能导致证明在不同应用对象间转移，除非维护额外的操作不变量

Method: 提出了系统安全分析方法：上下文绑定漏洞分类法、针对持有记录证明但无法获取新坐标的转录对手的离线电路验证形式模型、跨五种绑定策略类的假设比较，以及具体实现Zairn-ZKP，将投放点身份、策略版本和会话上下文作为公共电路输入嵌入

Result: 相比基于存储摘要服务器检查的强离线替代方案，证明内绑定将操作不变量从四个减少到两个，相对于仅地理的基线未增加可测量的证明成本（中位数-0.12毫秒）。还消除了一个经验识别的正确性陷阱：省略服务器端检查的合理离线实现仍易受跨投放点转移攻击。测量表明在密集城市部署中，除非维护每请求随机数，否则同周期转移是现实的

Conclusion: 结果支持一种可部署的方法论，用于减少状态化ZK支持验证工作流中的假设面。Zairn-ZKP通过将上下文绑定嵌入证明电路，减少了操作复杂性并提高了安全性

Abstract: A zero-knowledge proximity proof certifies geometric nearness but carries no commitment to an application context. In stateful geo-content systems, where drops can share coordinates, policies evolve, and content has persistent identity, this gap can permit proof transfer between application objects unless extra operational invariants are maintained. We present a systems-security analysis of this deployment problem: a taxonomy of context-binding vulnerabilities, a formal off-circuit verification model for a transcript-adversary that holds a recorded proof but cannot obtain fresh coordinates, an assumption comparison across five binding strategy classes, and a concrete instantiation, Zairn-ZKP, that embeds drop identity, policy version, and session context as public circuit inputs. Compared with a strong off-circuit alternative based on stored-digest server checking, in-proof binding reduces operational invariants from four to two and adds no measurable proving cost relative to the sound geo-only baseline (-0.12 ms median in our setup). It also removes a correctness pitfall we identify empirically: a plausible off-circuit implementation that omits one server-side check remains vulnerable to cross-drop transfer. Measurements across six network conditions, seven venues in four countries, and an epoch-window simulation indicate that same-epoch transfer is realistic in dense urban deployments unless per-request nonces are maintained. Across five platforms and seven binding strategies, the results support a deployable methodology for reducing assumption surfaces in stateful ZK-backed verification workflows.

</details>


### [146] [Search-Bound Proximity Proofs: Binding Encrypted Geographic Search to Zero-Knowledge Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03902)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种解决位置服务中搜索授权证明安全漏洞的方法，通过将搜索会话与零知识邻近证明绑定，防止审计时的错误关联攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结合加密地理搜索和零知识邻近证明的系统将两个阶段视为独立，在诚实但好奇的服务器模型下存在授权来源缺口：会话状态清除后，无法将证明追溯到其原始搜索会话，因为证明的公共输入不包含会话标识信息。

Method: 提出搜索绑定邻近证明（SBPP）方法，在不修改ZKP电路的情况下，将会话随机数、Merkle根结果集承诺和签名收据分解为可独立审计的组件，实现属性级故障隔离的离线审计。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据（110,776个OpenStreetMap POI）上的实验显示，在125毫秒的Groth16基准上，绝对开销低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: SBPP实现了搜索授权证明（SAP）安全要求，解决了授权来源缺口问题，通过可独立审计的组件设计支持离线审计中的属性级故障隔离。

Abstract: Location-based systems that combine encrypted geographic search with zero-knowledge proximity proofs typically treat the two phases as independent. Under an honest-but-curious server, this leaves an authorization provenance gap: once session state is purged, no forensic procedure can attribute a proof to its originating search session, because the proof's public inputs encode no session-identifying information.
  We formalize this gap as the search-authorized proof (SAP) security notion and show via a concrete audit re-association attack that proof-external mechanisms, where authorization evidence remains outside the proof, cannot prevent forensic misattribution when the same drop parameters recur across sessions. Search-Bound Proximity Proofs (SBPP) realize the SAP requirements without modifying the ZKP circuit: session nonce, Merkle-root result-set commitment, and signed receipt are decomposed into independently auditable components, enabling property-level fault isolation in offline audit. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data (110,776 OpenStreetMap POIs) show sub-millisecond absolute overhead on a 125 ms Groth16 baseline.

</details>


### [147] [Improving ML Attacks on LWE with Data Repetition and Stepwise Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03903)
*Alberto Alfarano,Eshika Saxena,Emily Wenger,François Charton,Kristin Lauter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了机器学习攻击在LWE问题中的应用，通过扩大训练集和重复样本，成功恢复了更密集的秘密，并提出了逐步回归技术来恢复"冷比特"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LWE问题是格密码学中的核心难题，现有ML攻击主要针对稀疏秘密（如二进制、三元和小秘密）有效。然而，对于更密集的秘密恢复能力有限，需要探索如何扩展ML攻击的有效范围。

Method: 采用更大的训练集和重复样本策略来增强模型训练；提出逐步回归技术来恢复秘密的"冷比特"；通过实验观察模型恢复尝试、数据集大小和重复样本之间的幂律关系。

Result: 实验表明，使用更大训练集和重复样本能够恢复更密集的秘密；发现了模型恢复尝试、数据集大小和重复样本之间的幂律关系；成功恢复了在"残酷区域"中具有最多3个活跃比特的秘密。

Conclusion: 通过扩大训练数据和采用重复样本策略，可以显著提升ML攻击对LWE问题中更密集秘密的恢复能力。逐步回归技术为恢复秘密的"冷比特"提供了有效方法，扩展了ML攻击在格密码分析中的应用范围。

Abstract: The Learning with Errors (LWE) problem is a hard math problem in lattice-based cryptography. In the simplest case of binary secrets, it is the subset sum problem, with error. Effective ML attacks on LWE were demonstrated in the case of binary, ternary, and small secrets, succeeding on fairly sparse secrets. The ML attacks recover secrets with up to 3 active bits in the "cruel region" (Nolte et al., 2024) on samples pre-processed with BKZ. We show that using larger training sets and repeated examples enables recovery of denser secrets. Empirically, we observe a power-law relationship between model-based attempts to recover the secrets, dataset size, and repeated examples. We introduce a stepwise regression technique to recover the "cool bits" of the secret.

</details>


### [148] [Enabling Deterministic User-Level Interrupts in Real-Time Processors via Hardware Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04015)
*Hongbin Yang,Huanle Zhang,Runyu Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种硬件扩展，允许用户级中断直接、确定性地切换到目标保护域，无需内核干预，将最坏情况延迟降低50倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时嵌入式系统需要将软件组件隔离到独立保护域以减少攻击面和限制故障传播，但应用提供的设备中断处理程序（即使是不可信的）必须留在内核中以最小化中断延迟，这破坏了安全性并增加了手动认证负担。现有硬件扩展仅在目标保护域被内核调度时才加速中断，只能改善平均性能而非最坏情况延迟，无法满足自动驾驶或机器人等关键实时应用的需求。

Method: 提出一种新颖的硬件扩展，使中断到达时能够直接、确定性地切换到适当的保护域，无需内核干预，即使该域处于休眠状态。该扩展通过硬件机制实现用户级中断的直接交付。

Result: 硬件扩展将最坏情况延迟降低50倍以上，核心面积增加19%（占总芯片面积的2%），动态功耗增加4.1%。这是首个保证用户级中断交付且具有纳秒级有界最坏情况延迟的集成机制。

Conclusion: 提出的硬件扩展解决了实时嵌入式系统中用户级中断交付的确定性延迟问题，为关键实时应用提供了安全且高性能的解决方案，首次实现了纳秒级有界最坏情况延迟的用户级中断机制。

Abstract: The growing complexity of real-time embedded systems demands strong isolation of software components into separate protection domains to reduce attack surfaces and limit fault propagation. However, application-supplied device interrupt handlers -- even untrusted -- have to remain in the kernel to minimize interrupt latency, undermining security and burdening manual certifications. Current hardware extensions accelerate interrupts only when the target protection domain is scheduled by the kernel; consequently, they are limited to improving average-case performance but not worst-case latency, and do not meet the requirements of critical real-time applications such as autonomous vehicles or robots.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel hardware extension that enables direct, deterministic switching to the appropriate protection domain upon user-level interrupt arrival -- without kernel intervention -- even when that domain is dormant. Our hardware extension reduces worst-case latency by more than 50x with a 19% increase in core area (2% of total die area) and 4.1% increase in dynamic power. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first integrated mechanism to guarantee user-level interrupt delivery with a nanosecond-scale yet bounded worst-case latency.

</details>


### [149] [CoopGuard: Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Round Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04060)
*Siyuan Li,Zehao Liu,Xi Lin,Qinghua Mao,Yuliang Chen,Haoyu Li,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Xiu Su*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CoopGuard：基于协作智能体的状态化多轮LLM防御框架，通过维护和更新内部防御状态来对抗演化攻击，在EMRA基准测试中显著降低攻击成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在复杂应用中的部署增加，其对抗攻击脆弱性引发安全担忧，特别是在多轮交互中的演化攻击。现有防御大多是被动的，难以适应攻击者在多轮中精炼策略的变化。

Method: 提出CoopGuard框架，基于协作智能体维护和更新内部防御状态。包含三个专用智能体（延迟智能体、诱惑智能体、取证智能体），由系统智能体协调，根据演化防御状态（交互历史）做出决策并随时间编排智能体。

Result: 在EMRA基准测试（包含5,200个对抗样本，涵盖8种攻击类型）中，CoopGuard将攻击成功率降低78.9%（相比最先进防御），欺骗率提高186%，攻击效率降低167.9%，提供更全面的多轮防御评估。

Conclusion: CoopGuard为多轮对抗场景中的LLM提供鲁棒保护，通过状态化多轮防御框架有效应对演化攻击，显著提升防御性能。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in complex applications, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks raises urgent safety concerns, especially those evolving over multi-round interactions. Existing defenses are largely reactive and struggle to adapt as adversaries refine strategies across rounds. In this work, we propose CoopGuard , a stateful multi-round LLM defense framework based on cooperative agents that maintains and updates an internal defense state to counter evolving attacks. It employs three specialized agents (Deferring Agent, Tempting Agent, and Forensic Agent) for complementary round-level strategies, coordinated by System Agent, which conditions decisions on the evolving defense state (interaction history) and orchestrates agents over time. To evaluate evolving threats, we introduce the EMRA benchmark with 5,200 adversarial samples across 8 attack types, simulating progressively LLM multi-round attacks. Experiments show that CoopGuard reduces attack success rate by 78.9% over state-of-the-art defenses, while improving deceptive rate by 186% and reducing attack efficiency by 167.9%, offering a more comprehensive assessment of multi-round defense. These results demonstrate that CoopGuard provides robust protection for LLMs in multi-round adversarial scenarios.

</details>


### [150] [Styx: Collaborative and Private Data Processing With TEE-Enforced Sticky Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04082)
*Shixuan Zhao,Weicheng Wang,Ninghui Li,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Styx框架通过结合粘性策略和可信执行环境，为多方不互信的数据协作提供安全、隐私保护且符合策略的数据处理环境


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数据驱动协作（如AI训练）中保护敏感信息同时满足多个互不信任利益相关者多样化需求的挑战

Method: 集成粘性策略与可信执行环境，使用硬件TEE保护的中间件和编程语言运行时创建沙盒环境，设计数据处理工作流和流水线实现数据使用保护、生命周期保护和动态协作

Result: Styx能够使协作计算（如联合AI训练）更安全、隐私保护且符合策略，性能开销在单节点计算中合理，并能扩展到大规模分布式多节点部署

Conclusion: Styx框架成功解决了多方不互信环境下的数据保护挑战，通过硬件TEE和策略执行的集成实现了端到端的数据安全保护

Abstract: Protecting sensitive information in data-driven collaborations, such as AI training, while meeting the diverse requirements of multiple mutually distrusted stakeholders, is both crucial and challenging. This paper presents Styx, a novel framework to address this challenge by integrating sticky policies with Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs). At a high level, Styx employs a hardware-TEE-protected middleware with a programming language runtime to form a sandboxed environment for both the data processing and policy enforcement. We carefully designed a data processing workflow and pipelines to enable a strong yet flexible data-specific policy enforcement throughout the entire data lifecycle and data derivation to achieve data-in-use protection, data lifecycle protection and dynamic collaboration. We implemented Styx and demonstrated its ability to make collaborative computing, such as joint AI training, more secure, privacy-preserving, and policy-compliant. Our evaluation shows the performance overheads imposed by Styx are reasonable on single-node computation with the capability to scale to a large distributed multi-node deployment.

</details>


### [151] [Invisible Adversaries: A Systematic Study of Session Manipulation Attacks on VPNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04099)
*Yuxiang Yang,Ao Wang,Xuewei Feng,Qi Li,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文发现了VPN连接跟踪框架中的多个漏洞，允许连接到同一VPN服务器的攻击者对其他用户发起DoS攻击、劫持TCP连接或注入伪造DNS响应，影响多个流行框架和商业VPN提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPN用户期望获得足够的安全保护，并且不受连接到同一VPN服务器的其他用户的影响，这体现为非干扰属性。然而，当前VPN服务器的连接跟踪框架存在共享资源滥用和会话状态转换验证不足的问题，可能违反这一安全属性。

Method: 研究人员识别了VPN连接跟踪框架中的漏洞，提出了三种针对通过VPN隧道的TCP和UDP流量的会话操纵攻击。对五个流行连接跟踪框架（跨不同操作系统）和九家主要商业VPN提供商进行了评估，验证了攻击的有效性。

Result: 实验结果显示，所有测试的连接跟踪框架和八家VPN提供商都至少对其中一种攻击存在漏洞。研究人员负责任地披露了发现并提出了缓解措施，获得了19个分配的CVE/CNVD编号，并得到了相关社区和提供商的认可。

Conclusion: VPN连接跟踪框架中的共享资源滥用和会话状态验证不足导致严重的安全漏洞，破坏了VPN的非干扰属性。这些发现强调了VPN基础设施需要更严格的安全审计和改进的会话管理机制。

Abstract: Virtual Private Networks (VPNs) are widely used for censorship evasion and traffic protection. VPN users expect to be provided with adequate security protection, and at the same time not be affected by other users connected to the same VPN server, which can be illustrated as the non-interference property. However, in this paper, we have identified several vulnerabilities that violate this property, specifically within the connection tracking frameworks of VPN servers, stemming from shared resource misuse and insufficient validation of session state transitions. We present three session manipulation attacks targeting TCP and UDP traffic tunneled through VPNs. The attacker who only connects to the same VPN server can launch denial-of-service attacks, hijack TCP connections of other clients, or inject forged DNS responses into their queries. We evaluate these attacks against five popular connection tracking frameworks across different OSes and nine major commercial VPN providers. Experimental results reveal that all frameworks and eight providers are vulnerable to at least one of the attacks. We have responsibly disclosed our findings with countermeasures, resulting in 19 assigned CVEs/CNVDs and acknowledgments from the communities and providers.

</details>


### [152] [Triggering and Detecting Exploitable Library Vulnerability from the Client by Directed Greybox Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04102)
*Yukai Zhao,Menghan Wu,Xing Hu,Shaohua Wang,Meng Luo,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LiveFuzz是一种基于定向灰盒模糊测试的新方法，用于在没有PoC的情况下从客户端程序检测库漏洞的可利用性，通过目标元组、抽象路径映射和基于风险的自适应变异来提高漏洞检测效果。


<details>
