  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖PoC来生成测试用例触发库漏洞，但PoC通常不可用。需要一种不依赖PoC就能从客户端程序检测库漏洞可利用性的方法。

Method: LiveFuzz基于定向灰盒模糊测试，引入目标元组将DGF扩展到跨程序场景；提出抽象路径映射机制来投影执行路径，缓解对短路径的偏好；采用基于风险的自适应变异来减少过度变异。

Result: 在包含61个库漏洞案例的数据集上评估，LiveFuzz相比所有基线方法增加了目标可达路径数量，提高了漏洞暴露的平均速度，并独家触发了三个漏洞。

Conclusion: LiveFuzz有效解决了不依赖PoC检测库漏洞的问题，通过创新的路径映射和变异策略显著提升了漏洞检测能力。

Abstract: Developers utilize third-party libraries to improve productivity, which also introduces potential security risks. Existing approaches generate tests for public functions to trigger library vulnerabilities from client programs, yet they depend on proof-of-concepts (PoCs), which are often unavailable. In this paper, we propose a new approach, LiveFuzz, based on directed greybox fuzzing (DGF) to detect the exploitability of library vulnerabilities from client programs without PoCs. LiveFuzz exploits a target tuple to extend existing DGF techniques to cross-program scenarios. Based on the target tuple, LiveFuzz introduces a novel Abstract Path Mapping mechanism to project execution paths, mitigating the preference for shorter paths. LiveFuzz also proposes a risk-based adaptive mutation to mitigate excessive mutation. To evaluate LiveFuzz, we construct a new dataset including 61 cases of library vulnerabilities exploited from client programs. Results show that LiveFuzz increases the number of target-reachable paths compared with all baselines and improves the average speed of vulnerability exposure. Three vulnerabilities are triggered exclusively by LiveFuzz.

</details>


### [153] [Beamforming Feedback as a Novel Attack Surface for Wi-Fi Physical-Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04179)
*Jingzhe Zhang,Yitong Shen,Ning Wang,Yili Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BFIAttack是一种利用Wi-Fi波束赋形反馈信息(BFI)重构合法用户CSI的新型攻击方法，可破坏Wi-Fi物理层安全，在单天线场景下单次攻击成功率超93%，多天线场景下5次内成功率73%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Wi-Fi技术从数据传输扩展到物理层安全应用，尽管已有大量基于CSI的物理层安全研究，但其安全漏洞尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在揭示Wi-Fi物理层安全中存在的关键漏洞。

Method: 提出BFIAttack攻击方法：1) 单天线场景使用闭式CSI重构方法；2) 多天线场景使用最大似然估计CSI重构；3) 利用天线对间的空间相似性优化重构CSI，提升攻击效果。

Result: 实验结果显示：多天线场景下不超过5次攻击尝试的平均攻击成功率为73%；单天线场景下单次攻击成功率超过93%。攻击成功证明了现有Wi-Fi物理层安全存在严重漏洞。

Conclusion: BFIAttack揭示了现有Wi-Fi物理层安全的关键漏洞，通过利用BFI重构CSI可有效破坏设备认证、用户认证和密钥生成等安全机制，需要重新评估和改进Wi-Fi物理层安全方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of wireless technologies, Wi-Fi has expanded beyond its original role in data transmission to support various emerging applications, particularly in physical-layer security, including device authentication, user authentication, and secret key generation. Despite extensive research on Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI)-based physical-layer security, its vulnerabilities remain largely unexplored. In this work, we propose BFIAttack, a novel attack that exploits Beamforming Feedback Information (BFI) to reconstruct the CSI of a legitimate user or device, thereby compromising Wi-Fi-based physical-layer security. We realize the attack by leveraging a closed-form CSI reconstruction method for the single-antenna station scenario and a maximum likelihood estimation-based CSI reconstruction for the multi-antenna station scenario. Moreover, we exploit spatial similarities among antenna pairs to refine the reconstructed CSI and enhance attack effectiveness. Experimental results show that BFIAttack achieves an average attack success rate of $73\%$ in multi-antenna station scenarios with no more than five attack attempts, and over $93\%$ in single-antenna station scenarios with only a single attempt. BFIAttack reveals critical vulnerabilities in existing Wi-Fi-based physical-layer security.

</details>


### [154] [Perils of Parallelism: Transaction Fee Mechanisms under Execution Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04193)
*Sarisht Wadhwa,Aviv Yaish,Fan Zhang,Kartik Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文分析了区块链并行执行环境下的交易费机制问题，揭示了现有机制在并行性和执行偶然性方面的安全漏洞，提出了一个理论框架并证明了不可能性结果，最后设计了达到权衡边界的费用机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代区块链依赖并行执行提高吞吐量，但现有交易费机制(TFMs)难以同时处理执行并行性、性能表现和公平性。当并行性影响费用时，会出现对抗性操纵：用户可插入无功能的并行交易降低费用，调度者可创建无用顺序交易增加收入。执行偶然性（表达性编程语言的核心特性）加剧了这些问题并引入新威胁。

Method: 引入一个框架来分析并行执行和偶然性环境下的交易费机制挑战，证明了一个不可能性定理，揭示了用户和调度者风险最小化之间的固有张力。为了完成分析，设计了新的费用机制并证明它们达到了这种权衡的边界。

Result: 揭示了现有TFMs在并行执行环境中的安全漏洞：1) 用户可策略性插入无用并行交易降低费用；2) 调度者可创建无用顺序交易增加收入；3) 执行偶然性导致用户为未使用资源超额支付；4) 调度者收入因预留调度槽未使用而受损。证明了不可能性结果，显示并行性和偶然性涉及用户与调度者风险最小化的权衡。

Conclusion: 论文为评估Sui和Monad等著名区块链提出的设计提供了严格的理论基础。结果表明，在并行执行和偶然性环境下，交易费机制设计面临固有的权衡挑战，而提出的新机制能够达到这种权衡的理论边界。

Abstract: Modern blockchains increasingly rely on parallel execution to improve throughput. We show several industry and academic transaction fee mechanisms (TFMs) struggle to simultaneously account for execution parallelism while remaining performant and fair. First, if parallelism affects fees, adversarial protocol manipulations that offset possible benefits to throughput by introducing fake transactions become rational: users can insert functionally useless parallel transactions solely to reduce fees, and schedulers can create useless sequential transactions to increase revenue. Execution contingency, a core feature of expressive programming languages, both exacerbates the aforementioned threats and introduces new ones: (1) users may overpay for unused resources, and (2) scheduler revenue is harmed when reserved scheduling slots go unused due to contingency. We introduce a framework for this challenging setting, and prove an impossibility, highlighting an inherent tension: both parallelism and contingency involve a trade-off between minimizing risks for users and schedulers, as favoring one comes at the expense of the other. To complete the picture, we introduce a fee mechanisms and prove that they achieve the boundaries of this trade-off. Our results provide rigorous foundations for evaluating designs advanced by notable blockchains, such as Sui and Monad.

</details>


### [155] [Governance-Constrained Agentic AI: Blockchain-Enforced Human Oversight for Safety-Critical Wildfire Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04265)
*Ali Akarma,Toqeer Ali Syed,Salman Jan,Hammad Muneer,Abdul Khadar Jilani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于区块链的治理感知AI架构，用于可信的野火早期预警系统，通过约束POMDP建模、分层多智能体协调和许可区块链层实现自适应监测、强制人工授权和可验证责任


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI野火监测系统缺乏自适应智能体协调、结构化人工控制和可验证责任机制，纯自主预警在安全关键灾害中可能导致误报、治理失败和系统信任缺失

Method: 1) 将野火监测建模为约束部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，考虑检测延迟、误报减少和资源消耗；2) 分层多智能体协调实现无人机动态风险自适应重分配；3) 许可区块链层通过智能合约设置强制人工授权作为状态转移不变量

Result: 安全分析显示系统抵抗警报注入、重放和篡改攻击；高保真仿真实验表明治理执行带来有限操作开销，减少误报并保持自适应检测性能

Conclusion: 该工作通过将问责制整合到需要安全性的灾害智能系统智能体控制循环中，为可靠AI系统的原则性设计范式迈出一步

Abstract: The AI-based sensing and autonomous monitoring have become the main components of wildfire early detection, but current systems do not provide adaptive inter-agent coordination, structurally defined human control, and cryptographically verifiable responsibility. Purely autonomous alert dissemination in the context of safety critical disasters poses threats of false alarming, governance failure and lack of trust in the system. This paper provides a blockchain-based governance-conscious agentic AI architecture of trusted wildfire early warning. The monitoring of wildfires is modeled as a constrained partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) that accounts for the detection latency, false alarms reduction and resource consumption with clear governance constraints. Hierarchical multi-agent coordination means dynamic risk-adaptive reallocation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). With risk-adaptive policies, a permissioned blockchain layer sets mandatory human-authorization as a state-transition invariant as a smart contract. We build formal assurances such as integrity of alerts, human control, non-repudiation and limited detection latency assumptions of Byzantine fault. Security analysis shows that it is resistant to alert injections, replays, and tampering attacks. High-fidelity simulation environment experimental evaluation of governance enforcement demonstrates that it presents limited operational overhead and decreases false public alerts and maintains adaptive detection performance. This work is a step towards a principled design paradigm of reliable AI systems by incorporating accountability into the agentic control loop of disaster intelligence systems that demand safety in their application.

</details>


### [156] [Semantics Over Syntax: Uncovering Pre-Authentication 5G Baseband Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04283)
*Qiqing Huang,Xingyu Wang,Wanda Guo,Guofei Gu,Hongxin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ConSeT框架通过提取5G规范级约束并生成语义违规测试用例，在商用和开源5G用户设备中发现了多个未公开漏洞，揭示了预认证信令中的语义不一致性是一个关键攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有5G UE测试主要关注语法无效输入，但规范级字段约束和跨字段依赖关系中的语义不一致性可能使基带实现进入无效状态，这构成了一个关键但未充分探索的攻击面。

Method: 提出Constraint-Guided Semantic Testing (ConSeT)框架：解码RRC消息为结构化字段，推导基于模式的规则，使用大型语言模型在证据受限方式下推断跨字段依赖关系，生成故意违反语义约束的语法有效测试用例。

Result: 在商用智能手机中发现7个先前未知漏洞（包括3个高危CVE），影响64种芯片组型号和542多个商用智能手机型号；在开源OAI UE中触发29个不同的崩溃点。

Conclusion: 语义不一致性在预认证信令中构成关键攻击面，ConSeT框架能有效发现5G UE实现中的漏洞，证明了基于规范约束的语义测试的重要性。

Abstract: Modern 5G user equipment (UE) processes Radio Resource Control (RRC) configuration messages during early control-plane exchanges, before authentication and integrity protection are established. Prior work for testing 5G UEs has largely focused on constructing syntactically invalid inputs. In contrast, we show that syntactically valid but semantically inconsistent messages, which violate specification-level field constraints or cross-field dependencies, can drive baseband implementations into invalid states, triggering assertion failures or modem crashes. These findings reveal semantic inconsistencies in pre-authentication signaling as a critical yet underexplored attack surface in 5G UE implementations. To address this gap, we present Constraint-Guided Semantic Testing (ConSeT), a framework that systematically extracts specification-level constraints and leverages them to generate targeted semantic violations for testing 5G UEs. ConSeT decodes RRC messages into structured fields, derives schema-based rules, infers cross-field dependencies using a Large Language Model (LLM) in an evidence-bounded manner, and produces syntactically valid test cases that intentionally violate semantic constraints. We evaluate ConSeT on both commercial and open-source 5G UEs. On commercial smartphones, it uncovers 7 previously unknown vulnerabilities through responsible disclosure, including 3 high-severity CVEs, affecting 64 chipset models and over 542 commercially available smartphone models. On the open-source OAI UE, ConSeT additionally triggers 29 distinct crash sites.

</details>


### [157] [Poisoned Identifiers Survive LLM Deobfuscation: A Case Study on Claude Opus 4.6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04289)
*Luis Guzmán Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在反混淆JavaScript时，即使模型理解正确语义，字符串表中的中毒标识符名称仍会持续出现在重构代码中，但任务框架改变可显著降低传播率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在反混淆JavaScript代码时，中毒标识符名称是否会在模型重构的代码中持续存在，即使模型已正确理解代码语义，以及任务框架如何影响这种传播

Method: 使用Claude Opus 4.6进行192次推理运行，测试两种代码原型（力导向图模拟和A*路径规划），共50种条件，每个条件N=3-6次重复。包括基线测试、匹配控制实验和不同任务框架（反混淆vs重新实现）的比较

Result: 1) 中毒名称在所有基线运行中持续存在（物理模拟：8/8；路径规划：5/5）。2) 持续存在与正确语义注释共存：15/17次运行中模型使用错误变量名但注释正确描述实际操作。3) 任务框架改变影响传播：显式验证提示无效（12/12），但将任务从"反混淆"改为"重新实现"将传播率从100%降至0-20%（物理）和0%（路径规划），同时保持算法结构

Conclusion: LLM在反混淆JavaScript时，中毒标识符名称会持续传播到重构代码中，即使模型理解正确语义。任务框架（反混淆vs重新实现）显著影响传播率，表明模型对代码表示和重构过程存在系统性偏差。研究结果基于两种代码原型和Claude模型家族，需要更广泛验证

Abstract: When an LLM deobfuscates JavaScript, can poisoned identifier names in the string table survive into the model's reconstructed code, even when the model demonstrably understands the correct semantics? Using Claude Opus 4.6 across 192 inference runs on two code archetypes (force-directed graph simulation, A* pathfinding; 50 conditions, N=3-6), we found three consistent patterns: (1) Poisoned names persisted in every baseline run on both artifacts (physics: 8/8; pathfinding: 5/5). Matched controls showed this extends to terms with zero semantic fit when the string table does not form a coherent alternative domain. (2) Persistence coexisted with correct semantic commentary: in 15/17 runs the model wrote wrong variable names while correctly describing the actual operation in comments. (3) Task framing changed persistence: explicit verification prompts had no effect (12/12 across 4 variants), but reframing from "deobfuscate this" to "write a fresh implementation" reduced propagation from 100% to 0-20% on physics and to 0% on pathfinding, while preserving the checked algorithmic structure. Matched-control experiments showed zero-fit terms persist at the same rate when the replacement table lacks a coherent alternative-domain signal. Per-term variation in earlier domain-gradient experiments is confounded with domain-level coherence and recoverability. These observations are from two archetypes on one model family (Opus 4.6 primary; Haiku 4.5 spot-check). Broader generalization is needed

</details>


### [158] [Evaluating Future Air Traffic Management Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04293)
*Konstantinos Spalas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了LDACS系统中基于PUF的身份验证机制的安全漏洞，包括PUF可预测性、量子计算威胁和老化问题，并提出PKI作为替代解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDACS系统旨在实现航空通信现代化，但现有的基于PUF的身份验证机制存在安全漏洞，包括PUF可预测性、量子计算威胁和老化问题，需要更可靠的替代方案。

Method: 分析基于PUF的LDACS身份验证机制，识别其潜在漏洞，包括：1）复杂模型可预测PUF响应；2）量子计算威胁当前密码学；3）PUF老化导致系统不稳定。在此基础上提出PKI作为替代方案。

Result: 研究发现基于PUF的LDACS身份验证机制存在严重安全风险：1）PUF可被预测模型攻击；2）量子计算可破解当前密码学；3）PUF老化导致系统不可用。这些漏洞使得攻击者可实施冒充攻击。

Conclusion: 基于PUF的LDACS身份验证机制存在多重安全漏洞，不适合航空通信安全需求。建议采用成熟的公钥基础设施（PKI）作为更可靠的替代解决方案。

Abstract: The L-Band Digital Aviation Communication System (LDACS) aims to modernize communications between the aircraft and the tower. Besides digitizing this type of communication, the contributors also focus on protecting them against cyberattacks. There are several proposals regarding LDACS security, and a recent one suggests the use of physical unclonable functions (PUFs) for the authentication module. This work demonstrates this PUF-based authentication mechanism along with its potential vulnerabilities. Sophisticated models are able to predict PUFs, and, on the other hand, quantum computers are capable of threatening current cryptography, consisting factors that jeopardize the authentication mechanism giving the ability to perform impersonation attacks. In addition, aging is a characteristic that affects the stability of PUFs, which may cause instability issues, rendering the system unavailable. In this context, this work proposes the well-established Public Key Infrastructure (PKI), as an alternative solution.

</details>


### [159] [DAO to (Anonymous) DAO Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04369)
*Minfeng Qi,Lin Zhong,Qin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Dao²框架解决了组织间区块链交易问题，通过分布式密钥派生、分布式隐身地址生成和门限签名技术，实现DAO到匿名DAO的安全交易，保持资金分布式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前区块链资产越来越多由组织而非个人控制，但现有支付机制仍针对单用户钱包，缺乏统一的组织间交易解决方案，特别是需要保持分布式控制和可选匿名性的场景。

Method: 提出Dao²框架，结合三个核心组件：分布式密钥派生（DKD）用于非隐身子地址生成，分布式隐身地址生成（DSAG）用于不可链接的一次性地址，门限签名用于授权。普通交易使用DKD，匿名交易使用DSAG，发送方通过门限签名支付，接收方无需重建主密钥即可赎回资金。

Result: 形式化证明了安全性，原型评估显示：典型7成员DAO的完整匿名DAO-to-DAO交易在27ms内完成，通信量小于1.2KB，且随DAO规模线性扩展。

Conclusion: Dao²为组织间区块链交易提供了统一解决方案，支持可选匿名性，保持分布式控制，具有高效性和可扩展性，填补了现有支付机制的空白。

Abstract: Blockchain assets are increasingly controlled by organizations rather than individuals. DAO treasuries, consortium wallets, and custodial exchanges rely on threshold authorization and multi-party key management, yet existing payment mechanisms still target single-user wallets, leaving no unified solution for organizational transfers. We formalize the problem of \emph{DAO-to-(anonymous)-DAO} transactions and present \textsc{Dao$^2$}, a framework that enables one threshold-controlled organization to pay another, optionally with recipient anonymity, while keeping received funds under distributed control. \textsc{Dao$^2$} combines three components: \emph{distributed key derivation} (DKD) for non-stealth child addresses, \emph{distributed stealth-address generation} (DSAG) for unlinkable one-time destinations, and \emph{threshold signatures} for authorization. For ordinary transfers, the receiver derives a non-stealth address via DKD; for anonymous transfers, it derives a stealth address via DSAG. The sender then threshold-signs the payment, and the receiver redeems the funds without reconstructing any master secret. We formally prove its security and evaluate a prototype. A complete anonymous DAO-to-DAO transaction for a typical-sized (e.g., 7-member) DAO finishes in under 27\,ms with less than 1.2\,KB of communication, and scales linearly with DAO size.

</details>


### [160] [Digital Privacy in IoT: Exploring Challenges, Approaches and Open Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04572)
*Shini Girija,Pranav M. Pawar,Raja Muthalagu,Mithun Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了物联网(IoT)生态系统中的数字隐私风险，提出了基于IEEE数字隐私模型的五类风险分类框架，并介绍了AURA-IoT这一多层框架来应对AI驱动的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网在医疗、智慧城市和家庭自动化等领域的广泛应用带来了严重的敏感数据安全挑战，特别是COVID-19大流行加速了物联网采用，加剧了隐私和数据保护问题。需要保护个人可识别信息(PII)并维护数字独立性权利。

Method: 采用IEEE数字隐私模型构建分类框架，将隐私风险分为五类：身份导向风险、行为风险、推断风险、数据操纵风险和监管风险。系统回顾现有隐私增强技术(PETs)，包括加密技术、区块链、联邦学习、差分隐私、强化学习、AI和动态同意机制。提出AURA-IoT多层框架，整合对抗鲁棒性、可解释性、透明度、公平性、合规性、动态同意和政策执行机制。

Result: 建立了全面的物联网数字隐私风险分类体系，展示了各种隐私增强技术在数据保密性、访问控制和信任管理方面的应用。AURA-IoT框架为解决AI驱动的隐私风险提供了系统化方案，整合了多种技术和管理机制。

Conclusion: 物联网数字隐私保护需要综合性的技术和管理方法。AURA-IoT框架展示了如何通过多层结构应对复杂隐私挑战。未来研究方向包括AI与加密隐私解决方案的集成，以实现物联网系统的全面数字隐私保护。

Abstract: Privacy has always been a critical issue in the digital era, particularly with the increasing use of Internet of Things (IoT) devices. As the IoT continues to transform industries such as healthcare, smart cities, and home automation, it has also introduced serious challenges regarding the security of sensitive and private data. This paper examines the complex landscape of digital privacy in IoT ecosystems, highlighting the need to protect personally identifiable information (PII) of individuals and uphold their rights to digital independence. Global events, such as the COVID-19 pandemic, have accelerated the adoption of IoT, raising concerns about privacy and data protection. This paper provides an in-depth examination of digital privacy risks in the IoT domain and introduces a clear taxonomy for evaluating them using the IEEE Digital Privacy Model. The proposed framework categorizes privacy risks into five types: identity-oriented, behavioral, inference, data manipulation, and regulatory risks. We review existing digital privacy solutions, including encryption technologies, blockchain, federated learning, differential privacy, reinforcement learning, AI, and dynamic consent mechanisms, to mitigate these risks. We also highlight how these privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs) help with data confidentiality, access control, and trust management. Additionally, this study presents AURA-IoT, a futuristic framework that tackles AI-driven privacy risks through a multi-layered structure. AURA-IoT integrates adversarial robustness, explainability, transparency, fairness, compliance, dynamic consent, and policy enforcement mechanisms to ensure digital privacy, security, and accountable IoT operations. Finally, we discuss ongoing challenges and potential research directions for integrating AI and encryption-based privacy solutions to achieve comprehensive digital privacy in future IoT systems.

</details>


### [161] [Packing Entries to Diagonals for Homomorphic Sparse-Matrix Vector Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04683)
*Kemal Mutluergil,Deniz Elbek,Kamer Kaya,Erkay Savaş*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何通过行/列置换将稀疏矩阵的非零元素压缩到尽可能少的循环对角线中，以降低同态加密中稀疏矩阵向量乘法的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密（HE）允许在加密数据上进行计算，但会带来显著开销。对于稀疏矩阵向量乘法，广泛使用的Halevi-Shoup方案的成本与占用的循环对角线数量线性相关，而由于矩阵的非零模式不规则，这些对角线数量可能很多。因此需要优化矩阵排列以减少对角线数量。

Method: 1. 形式化二维对角线打包问题（2DPP）并引入二维循环带宽度量；2. 为小实例提供整数规划公式；3. 针对大矩阵提出实用排序启发式算法，结合基于图的初始排序（带宽减少、反带宽最大化、谱分析）和基于迭代改进的优化阶段（使用2OPT和3OPT交换）；4. 引入密集行/列消除策略和HE感知成本模型。

Result: 在SuiteSparse集合的175个稀疏矩阵上实验表明：排序优化变体平均可将对角线数量减少5.5倍（其中一个实例减少45.6倍）。密集行/列消除方法在置换技术不足时特别有效，在一个案例中，消除策略将加密乘法成本额外降低了23.7倍（而无消除时仅改进1.9倍）。

Conclusion: 通过行/列置换优化稀疏矩阵的循环对角线打包，可显著降低同态加密中稀疏矩阵向量乘法的计算成本。提出的排序启发式算法和密集结构消除策略为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables computation over encrypted data but incurs a substantial overhead. For sparse-matrix vector multiplication, the widely used Halevi and Shoup (2014) scheme has a cost linear in the number of occupied cyclic diagonals, which may be many due to the irregular nonzero pattern of the matrix. In this work, we study how to permute the rows and columns of a sparse matrix so that its nonzeros are packed into as few cyclic diagonals as possible. We formalise this as the two-dimensional diagonal packing problem (2DPP), introduce the two-dimensional circular bandsize metric, and give an integer programming formulation that yields optimal solutions for small instances. For large matrices, we propose practical ordering heuristics that combine graph-based initial orderings - based on bandwidth reduction, anti-bandwidth maximisation, and spectral analysis - and an iterative-improvement-based optimization phase employing 2OPT and 3OPT swaps. We also introduce a dense row/column elimination strategy and an HE-aware cost model that quantifies the benefits of isolating dense structures. Experiments on 175 sparse matrices from the SuiteSparse collection show that our ordering-optimisation variants can reduce the diagonal count by $5.5\times$ on average ($45.6\times$ for one instance). In addition, the dense row/column elimination approach can be useful for cases where the proposed permutation techniques are not sufficient; for instance, in one case, the additional elimination helped to reduce the encrypted multiplication cost by $23.7\times$ whereas without elimination, the improvement was only $1.9\times$.

</details>


### [162] [GPIR: Enabling Practical Private Information Retrieval with GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04696)
*Hyesung Ji,Hyunah Yu,Jongmin Kim,Wonseok Choi,G. Edward Suh,Jung Ho Ahn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GPIR：一种GPU加速的私有信息检索系统，通过重新设计内核、数据布局和执行调度，解决了多客户端批处理中的性能瓶颈，相比现有GPU实现实现了高达305.7倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有信息检索（PIR）协议存在服务器端计算和内存流量大的问题。虽然GPU理论上适合处理PIR的计算密集型任务（如NTT和GEMM），但多客户端批处理会改变性能瓶颈：虽然分摊了数据库访问成本，却使工作集超出L2缓存容量，导致内存行为发散和DRAM流量过大。

Method: 提出了GPIR系统，包含三个关键技术：1）阶段感知的混合执行模型，在操作级内核（单独执行每个原始操作）和阶段级内核（将协议阶段内的所有操作融合到单个内核中以最大化片上数据重用）之间动态切换；2）针对RowSel阶段，通过转置布局的GEMM设计和细粒度流水线解决NTT驱动数据布局与分块GEMM访问模式之间的结构不匹配问题；3）将系统扩展到多GPU环境，以最小通信开销同时提升查询吞吐量和数据库容量。

Result: GPIR实现了高达305.7倍于当前最先进GPU实现（PIRonGPU）的吞吐量提升，并成功扩展到多GPU系统，显著提升了查询吞吐量和数据库容量。

Conclusion: 通过重新思考GPU上的PIR内核设计、数据布局和执行调度，GPIR有效解决了多客户端批处理带来的性能瓶颈，实现了显著的性能提升，为大规模PIR系统的GPU加速提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Private information retrieval (PIR) allows private database queries but is hindered by intense server-side computation and memory traffic. Modern lattice-based PIR protocols typically involve three phases: ExpandQuery (expanding a query into encrypted indices), RowSel (encrypted row selection), and ColTor (recursive "column tournament" for final selection). ExpandQuery and ColTor primarily perform number-theoretic transforms (NTTs), whereas RowSel reduces to large-scale independent matrix-matrix multiplications (GEMMs). GPUs are theoretically ideal for these tasks, provided multi-client batching is used to achieve high throughput. However, batching fundamentally reshapes performance bottlenecks; while it amortizes database access costs, it expands working sets beyond the L2 cache capacity, causing divergent memory behaviors and excessive DRAM traffic.
  We present GPIR, a GPU-accelerated PIR system that rethinks kernel design, data layout, and execution scheduling. We introduce a stage-aware hybrid execution model that dynamically switches between operation-level kernels, which execute each primitive operation separately, and stage-level kernels, which fuse all operations within a protocol stage into a single kernel to maximize on-chip data reuse. For RowSel, we identify a performance gap caused by a structural mismatch between NTT-driven data layouts and tiled GEMM access patterns, which is exacerbated by multi-client batching. We resolve this through a transposed-layout GEMM design and fine-grained pipelining. Finally, we extend GPIR to multi-GPU systems, scaling both query throughput and database capacity with negligible communication overhead. GPIR achieves up to 305.7x higher throughput than PIRonGPU, the state-of-the-art GPU implementation.

</details>


### [163] [Bridging Safety and Security in Complex Systems: A Model-Based Approach with SAFT-GT Toolchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04705)
*Irdin Pekaric,Raffaela Groner,Alexander Raschke,Thomas Witte,Jubril Gbolahan Adigun,Michael Felderer,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAFT-GT工具链为自适应系统提供安全与安全保障，通过攻击-故障树生成和模型组合方法，集成到现有系统中，无需大量修改和领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程快速发展中，对鲁棒和安全系统的需求日益增长，特别是自适应系统因其复杂性和动态运行环境面临更大挑战，需要同时确保安全和安全性的解决方案。

Method: 设计开发SAFT-GT工具链，采用攻击-故障树生成和模型组合方法，能够无缝集成到现有系统中，特别集成到自适应系统的反馈循环中，支持处理已知漏洞和新兴攻击向量。

Result: 通过包含领域专家的广泛用户研究验证了工具链的实际适用性，证明其在现实场景中的相关性和可用性，同时识别了未来改进方向。工具链已开源以促进可重复性和进一步研究。

Conclusion: SAFT-GT工具链有效解决了自适应系统的安全与安全挑战，在实际应用中表现出色，为未来研究和改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the rapidly evolving landscape of software engineering, the demand for robust and secure systems has become increasingly critical. This is especially true for self-adaptive systems due to their complexity and the dynamic environments in which they operate. To address this issue, we designed and developed the SAFT-GT toolchain that tackles the multifaceted challenges associated with ensuring both safety and security. This paper provides a comprehensive description of the toolchain's architecture and functionalities, including the Attack-Fault Trees generation and model combination approaches. We emphasize the toolchain's ability to integrate seamlessly with existing systems, allowing for enhanced safety and security analyses without requiring extensive modifications and domain knowledge. Our proposed approach can address evolving security threats, including both known vulnerabilities and emerging attack vectors that could compromise the system. As a use case for the toolchain, we integrate it into the feedback loop of self-adaptive systems. Finally, to validate the practical applicability of the toolchain, we conducted an extensive user study involving domain experts, whose insights and feedback underscore the toolchain's relevance and usability in real-world scenarios. Our findings demonstrate the toolchain's effectiveness in real-world applications while highlighting areas for future improvements. The toolchain and associated resources are available in an open-source repository to promote reproducibility and encourage further research in this field.

</details>


### [164] [Hardware-Level Governance of AI Compute: A Feasibility Taxonomy for Regulatory Compliance and Treaty Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04712)
*Samar Ansari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个包含20种硬件级治理机制的分类法，评估了其在AI治理中的技术可行性，并揭示了条约验证所需机制与当前技术成熟度之间的结构性不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI治理主要依赖计算资源控制，但政策提案中涉及的硬件级机制缺乏从工程角度的系统分析。需要弥合AI治理与计算机工程之间的鸿沟，为政策制定者提供技术基础。

Method: 提出包含20种硬件级治理机制的分类法，按功能（监控、验证、执行）组织，采用四点量表评估技术可行性，分析对抗性漏洞，并将机制映射到四种治理场景。

Result: 发现结构性不匹配：条约验证最需要的机制（片上计算计量、密码学训练证明、硬件嵌入式执行）技术最不成熟。识别了基于计算治理的主要威胁和时态约束。

Conclusion: 硬件级治理需要类似IAEA核查的防篡改保证而非绝对防篡改。分类法、可行性评估和场景映射为政策制定者提供了技术基础，并指明了支持可验证国际协议所需的研发投资。

Abstract: The governance of frontier AI increasingly relies on controlling access to computational resources, yet the hardware-level mechanisms invoked by policy proposals remain largely unexamined from an engineering perspective. This paper bridges the gap between AI governance and computer engineering by proposing a taxonomy of 20 hardware-level governance mechanisms, organised by function (monitoring, verification, enforcement) and assessed for technical feasibility on a four-point scale from currently deployable to speculative. For each mechanism, we provide a technical description, a feasibility rating, and an identification of adversarial vulnerabilities. We map the taxonomy onto four governance scenarios: domestic regulation, bilateral agreements, multilateral treaty verification, and industry self-regulation. Our analysis reveals a structural mismatch: the mechanisms most needed for treaty verification, including on-chip compute metering, cryptographic proof-of-training, and hardware-embedded enforcement, are also the least mature. We assess principal threats to compute-based governance, including algorithmic efficiency gains, distributed training methods, and sovereignty concerns. We identify a temporal constraint: the window during which semiconductor manufacturing concentration makes hardware-level governance implementable is narrowing, while R&D timelines for critical mechanisms span years. We present an adversary-tiered threat analysis distinguishing commercial, non-state, and nation-state actors, arguing the appropriate security standard is tamper-evident assurance analogous to IAEA verification rather than absolute tamper-proofing. The taxonomy, feasibility classification, and mechanism-to-scenario mapping provide a technical foundation for policymakers and identify the R&D investments required before hardware-level governance can support verifiable international agreements.

</details>


### [165] [RegGuard: Legitimacy and Fairness Enforcement for Optimistic Rollups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04748)
*Zhenhang Shang,Yingzhe Yu,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RegGuard框架通过语义验证、跨层状态同步和公平排序服务，为乐观Rollup提供监管合规性保障，减少90%结算失败，保持85%吞吐量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐观Rollup虽然提供可扩展的智能合约执行，但由于语义合法性、跨层状态一致性和排序公平性三个结构性缺陷，不适合受监管的金融应用

Method: RegGuard集成三个协调机制：1) 基于RegSpec规则语言的可判定语义验证器；2) 具有概率可靠性边界的跨层状态预同步验证器；3) 具有可忽略违规概率的密码学可验证公平排序服务

Result: 在Optimism-based Rollup上实现15,000行原型，在对抗条件下评估：减少90%以上结算失败，防止可检测的排序操纵，保持85%基线吞吐量

Conclusion: RegGuard为乐观Rollup提供全面的合法性保证，填补了监管金融应用的结构性空白，在保持性能的同时显著提升安全性和合规性

Abstract: Optimistic rollups provide scalable smart-contract execution but remain unsuitable for regulated financial applications due to three structural gaps: semantic legitimacy, cross-layer state consistency, and ordering fairness. We introduce RegGuard, a unified framework that enhances optimistic rollups with comprehensive legitimacy guarantees. RegGuard integrates three coordinated mechanisms: a decidable semantic validator powered by the RegSpec rule language for encoding regulatory constraints; a cross-layer state pre-synchronization validator that detects inconsistent L1-L2 dependencies with probabilistic reliability bounds; and a cryptographically verifiable fair-ordering service that ensures transaction sequencing fairness with negligible violation probability. We implement a 15,000-line prototype integrated into an Optimism-based rollup and evaluate it under adversarial conditions. RegGuard reduces settlement failures by over 90%, prevents detectable ordering manipulation, and maintains 85% of baseline throughput.

</details>


### [166] [GPU Acceleration of TFHE-Based High-Precision Nonlinear Layers for Encrypted LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04783)
*Guoci Chen,Xiurui Pan,Qiao Li,Bo Mao,Congming Gao,Chengying Huan,Mingzhe Zhang,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TIGER是首个GPU加速的高精度TFHE非线性LLM层评估框架，通过GPU优化的WoP-PBS方法和数值算法突破查找表精度限制，实现高效加密推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM云服务部署存在隐私泄露风险，现有FHE方法在非线性函数评估上效率低且精度不足：CKKS需要高次多项式近似成本高，TFHE的PBS虽能精确评估但缺乏高精度实现且未充分利用GPU资源。

Method: 提出TIGER框架：1) GPU优化的WoP-PBS方法结合数值算法突破非线性函数的查找表精度限制；2) 实现关键非线性层的高精度高效实现；3) 采用批处理驱动设计利用输入间并行性提升GPU效率。

Result: 相比CPU基线，TIGER在GELU、Softmax和LayerNorm上分别实现7.17×、16.68×和17.05×的加速比，显著提升加密推理性能。

Conclusion: TIGER是首个GPU加速的高精度TFHE非线性LLM层评估框架，通过创新方法解决了现有FHE在LLM非线性层评估中的效率与精度瓶颈，为实用化加密推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) as cloud services raises privacy concerns as inference may leak sensitive data. Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) allows computation on encrypted data, but current FHE methods struggle with efficient and precise nonlinear function evaluation. Specifically, CKKS-based approaches require high-degree polynomial approximations, which are costly when target precision increases. Alternatively, TFHE's Programmable Bootstrapping (PBS) outperforms CKKS by offering exact lookup-table evaluation. But it lacks high-precision implementations of LLM nonlinear layers and underutilizes GPU resources.
  We propose \emph{TIGER}, the first GPU-accelerated framework for high-precision TFHE-based nonlinear LLM layer evaluation. TIGER offers: (1) GPU-optimized WoP-PBS method combined with numerical algorithms to surpass native lookup-table precision limits on nonlinear functions; (2) high-precision and efficient implementations of key nonlinear layers, enabling practical encrypted inference; (3) batch-driven design exploiting inter-input parallelism to boost GPU efficiency. TIGER achieves 7.17$\times$, 16.68$\times$, and 17.05$\times$ speedups over a CPU baseline for GELU, Softmax, and LayerNorm, respectively.

</details>


### [167] [Cryptanalysis of the Legendre Pseudorandom Function over Extension Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04833)
*Daksh Pandey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对扩展域$\mathbb{F}_{p^r}$上的单次Legendre PRF进行系统密码分析，揭示了两种攻击方法：被动模型下的差分签名分桶攻击和主动模型下的几何序列攻击，证明了需要更高次密钥变体才能实现指数级安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Legendre PRF因其在MPC和ZKP协议中的低乘法复杂度而备受重视，但其在扩展域$\mathbb{F}_{p^r}$上的安全性尚未得到充分研究。随着扩展域实例化的兴起，需要系统分析其安全边界。

Method: 1. 被动威胁模型分析：针对多项式输入编码，利用"无进位断裂"的确定性周期性，提出"差分签名"分桶技术，通过结构形状分组绕过防御。2. 主动威胁模型分析：使用原始多项式生成的几何序列评估PRF，利用$\mathbb{F}^*_{p^r}$上的严格乘法同态性，将现有表碰撞攻击推广到扩展域。

Result: 1. 被动攻击可在$\mathcal{O}(U \cdot p^r/M)$操作内恢复密钥，其中$U$是唯一性距离。2. 主动攻击可在$\mathcal{O}(p^r/M)$操作内提取密钥。3. 证明了单次Legendre PRF在扩展域上存在结构性弱点，需要至少二次密钥变体才能抵抗结构性约简攻击。

Conclusion: 扩展域$\mathbb{F}_{p^r}$上的单次Legendre PRF易受结构性攻击，其安全边界有限。为确保指数级安全性，必须采用更高次密钥变体（$d \ge 2$），这为未来安全Legendre PRF设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The Legendre Pseudorandom Function (PRF) is a highly efficient cryptographic primitive built upon the Legendre symbol, valued for its low multiplicative complexity in Multi-Party Computation (MPC) and Zero-Knowledge Proof (ZKP) protocols. While its security over prime fields $\mathbb{F}_p$ is well-documented, recent interest has shifted toward instantiations over extension fields $\mathbb{F}_{p^r}$. This paper presents the first comprehensive cryptanalysis of the single-degree Legendre PRF operating over $\mathbb{F}_{p^r}$.
  First, we analyze polynomial input encoding under a standard passive threat model (sequential additive counter queries). We demonstrate that while the absence of polynomial carry-overs causes an asynchronous "no-carry fracture" that neutralizes classical sliding-window collision attacks, the fracture itself is deterministically periodic. By introducing a novel "Differential Signature" bucketing technique, we prove that an adversary can systematically group fractured sequences by their structural shapes to bypass this defense, recovering the secret key in $\mathcal{O}(U \cdot p^r/M)$ operations, where $U$ is the unicity distance.
  Second, we evaluate the PRF under an active Chosen-Query threat model. We demonstrate that an adversary can circumvent the additive fracture by evaluating the PRF along a geometric sequence generated by a primitive polynomial. This structure invokes strict multiplicative homomorphism over $\mathbb{F}^*_{p^r}$, permitting a direct generalization of state-of-the-art table collision attacks to extract the key in $\mathcal{O}(p^r/M)$ operations. Finally, we establish the cryptographic boundaries of these attacks, formally proving the necessity of higher-degree key variants ($d \ge 2$) to achieve exponential security against structural reduction in extension fields.

</details>


### [168] [Comprehensive List of User Deception Techniques in Emails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04926)
*Maxime Veit,Mattia Mossano,Tobias Länge,Melanie Volkamer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个包含42种电子邮件欺骗技术的结构化列表，配有64个具体实现示例，围绕发件人、链接、附件安全指示器以及针对邮件渲染环境的技术进行组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子邮件作为核心通信媒介，其长期存在的设计和界面惯例仍然容易受到欺骗攻击。研究旨在系统化地识别和记录电子邮件欺骗技术，为未来的防范措施提供结构化参考。

Method: 基于先前的系统性文献综述，整合先前报告的技术并添加新开发的示例实现，通过自主检查引入新颖的欺骗技术。每个条目单独解释底层机制，将高级欺骗目标与具体技术实现分离。

Result: 创建了一个包含42种电子邮件欺骗技术的结构化列表，配有64个具体实现示例，围绕发件人、链接、附件安全指示器以及针对邮件渲染环境的技术进行组织。

Conclusion: 记录的欺骗技术可作为模块化构建块和结构化参考，支持未来在基础设施、邮件客户端设计和安全意识方面的对策研究，为研究人员、开发人员、操作人员和设计人员提供实用工具。

Abstract: Email remains a central communication medium, yet its long-standing design and interface conventions continue to enable deceptive attacks. This research note presents a structured list of 42 email-based deception techniques, documented with 64 concrete example implementations, organized around the sender, link, and attachment security indicators as well as techniques targeting the email rendering environment. Building on a prior systematic literature review, we consolidate previously reported techniques with newly developed example implementations and introduce novel deception techniques identified through our own examination. Rather than assessing effectiveness or real-world severity, each entry explains the underlying mechanism in isolation, separating the high-level deception goal from its concrete technical implementation. The documented techniques serve as modular building blocks and a structured reference for future work on countermeasures across infrastructure, email client design, and security awareness, supporting researchers as well as developers, operators, and designers working in these areas.

</details>
